- `cap_id`: the ID of the one `PartnerCap` that may set `recipient`. A
  `delayed::Delayed<CapKey, ID>` value.

A `delayed::Delayed<K, T>` comes from Blast's shared administration package
`blast_admin` and has five fields in BCS order: `parent` (the `Partner`'s ID),
`key` (`blast_partners::blast_partners::ShareKey` or
`blast_partners::blast_partners::CapKey`, each a one-byte unit struct), `value`
(the applied value), `delay_ms` (the notice), and `pending: Option<Proposal<T>>`.
A `delayed::Proposal<T>` holds `value`, `delay_ms` and `ready_at_ms`. The applied
value is always `value`; a proposal changes nothing until it is applied, even after
`ready_at_ms`. Both notices are 604,800,000 ms (seven days) and stay fixed:
`blast_partners` exposes no call that changes them.

Who may call: *anyone* may call `new_cap`, `destroy_cap` (on a cap they hold),
`pay`, `apply_share`, `apply_cap`, `delay_ms`, `max_share_bps`, and the readers
under Reading state; the *cap holder* (the applied
`PartnerCap`) may call `set_recipient`; a *Blast admin* with the `PartnerAdmin`
role (through `blast_acl::sign_in`) may call `register`, `schedule_share`,
`schedule_cap`, `cancel_share`, and `cancel_cap`.
Each product keeps its own list of the partners it accepts, managed by that
product's admin.

### Lifecycle

**Cap.** `blast_partners::blast_partners::new_cap` mints an inert cap: it
controls nothing until Blast registers a partner under its ID. It is *live* for
a partner while it is that partner's applied cap. After
`blast_partners::blast_partners::schedule_cap` proposes a different cap and seven
days pass, anyone may call `blast_partners::blast_partners::apply_cap`; only then
does the old cap stop working for that partner.
`blast_partners::blast_partners::destroy_cap` deletes a cap, live or not;
destroying a live cap leaves its partners with no working cap until a cap Blast
proposes with `blast_partners::blast_partners::schedule_cap` is applied, at
least seven days later.

**Partner.** `blast_partners::blast_partners::register` creates and shares the
partner with the agreed share and managing cap in force immediately. Registration
needs no `Clock`; there is no initial waiting period. From then on:

1. Blast proposes a new share or cap; the applied value stays in force.
2. A different proposal replaces the pending one and restarts the full seven
   days, even when the old proposal was already ready. Proposing the applied
   value, or the value already pending, aborts.
3. Blast may discard a proposal with `blast_partners::blast_partners::cancel_share`
   or `blast_partners::blast_partners::cancel_cap`, even after it is ready.
4. From `ready_at_ms`, anyone may call `blast_partners::blast_partners::apply_share`
   or `blast_partners::blast_partners::apply_cap`, the partner included. Nothing
   changes merely because time passes. `blast_partners::blast_partners::is_share_ready`
   and `blast_partners::blast_partners::is_cap_ready` say whether that call would
   succeed now.

There is no delete: a partner exists forever, because launches, presales, and
vaults bind its ID for good and pay Blast's held fees only with it. To switch it
off, Blast proposes and then applies a zero share; products that name it keep
working and simply pay it nothing.

**Product acceptance.** Each product accepts a partner in its own shared config,
mapping the partner's ID to the time it stops being accepted (`u64::MAX` until
removed): a dynamic field keyed by `PartnerKey(partner_id)`, or, for the presale,
the `acceptance_ends_at_ms` table of a `blast_partners::partner_list::PartnerList`:

| Product | Config object and key | Admin |
| --- | --- | --- |
| Boosts | `blast_boosts_aftermath::blast_boosts_aftermath::Protocol`, `blast_boosts_aftermath::blast_boosts_aftermath::PartnerKey` | `RiskAdmin` role (`operations::add_partner`, `operations::remove_partner`) |
| Presale | `creation_policy::CreationPolicy`, its `partners` `blast_partners::partner_list::PartnerList` | core `FeeAdmin` role (`creation_policy::add_partner`, `creation_policy::remove_partner`) |

Adding applies at once (and cancels a pending removal). Removing sets the value
to `now` plus `blast_partners::blast_partners::delay_ms()`: the partner stays
accepted for seven more days. A partner is accepted while the field exists and
`now < value`.

**Shared acceptance list.** `blast_partners::partner_list::PartnerList` is the
same acceptance rule as one embeddable value, for a product to keep inside its
own config. The launchpad embeds one in `launch_config::LaunchConfig` (its
`partners` field), changed by the `FeeAdmin` role through
`launch_config::add_partner` and `launch_config::remove_partner`. It maps each partner's ID to the time, in Unix epoch milliseconds,
it stops being accepted (`u64::MAX` until removed), held in a `Table`, and
records the `parent` object that embeds it. It has no authority of its own: only
the embedding module can reach it mutably, so that product decides who may
change it.

- `blast_partners::partner_list::new` creates an empty list for a parent.
- `blast_partners::partner_list::add` accepts a `Partner` at once, cancelling a
  pending removal or accepting a removed partner again; it aborts when the
  partner is already accepted with no removal pending.
- `blast_partners::partner_list::remove` keeps a partner accepted until `now`
  plus `delay_ms()`; it aborts unless the partner is accepted now with no
  removal pending.
- `blast_partners::partner_list::assert_accepted` aborts unless the list
  accepts the partner now; `blast_partners::partner_list::is_accepted` returns
  the same answer as a `bool`.
- `blast_partners::partner_list::contains` says whether the list has an entry
  for a partner (accepted, removal pending, or removed);
  `blast_partners::partner_list::acceptance_ends_at_ms` returns that entry's end
  time and aborts in `sui::dynamic_field` without one;
  `blast_partners::partner_list::length` counts entries, removed ones included;
  `blast_partners::partner_list::parent` returns the embedding object's ID.
- `blast_partners::partner_list::destroy` deletes the list with every entry and
  emits `PartnerListDestroyed`. It ends every acceptance at once, without the
  notice, so a product destroys it only with the object that embeds it.

### Flows

#### Become a partner (company)

1. Call `blast_partners::blast_partners::new_cap` (*anyone*). It returns a
   `PartnerCap`; send it with a TransferObjects command to the address that will
   manage your deals (for example your multisig).
2. Give Blast the cap's object ID, the recipient address, and the agreed share.
3. Blast's partner admin, in one PTB: `blast_acl::sign_in` with type argument
   `blast_partners::blast_partners::PartnerAdmin`, passing the shared `ACL` and
   its owned `AdminCap`; then `blast_partners::blast_partners::register` with a
   reference to that `AdminWitness<PartnerAdmin>`, your cap ID, the recipient,
   and the share. The new `Partner` is shared; its ID is in the `PartnerUpdated`
   event and the transaction's created objects. Its initial share is active immediately.
   Registration also emits `delayed::DelayedCreated` for the share and for the cap.
4. Blast's admin for each product adds the partner to that product's list.

`blast_partners::blast_partners::register` accepts any ID as the cap. Confirm on
chain that the `Partner`'s applied `cap_id.value` equals your cap's ID; a wrong
ID can only be fixed by proposing a new cap and applying it seven days later.

#### Change where a partner pays you (cap holder)

1. Call `blast_partners::blast_partners::set_recipient` with the shared
   `Partner` (mutable), your owned `PartnerCap` (by reference), and the new
   address. It takes no `Clock`.
2. It applies at once and emits `PartnerUpdated`. One call per partner.

#### Change a share or replace a lost cap (Blast admin)

1. `blast_acl::sign_in` with `blast_partners::blast_partners::PartnerAdmin`.
2. `blast_partners::blast_partners::schedule_share` (new share) or
   `blast_partners::blast_partners::schedule_cap` (new cap ID), with the shared
   `Partner`, the witness, the value, and `Clock`.
3. Each emits `delayed::DelayedScheduled` (after `delayed::DelayedCancelled`
   when it replaces a pending proposal) with `ready_at_ms`, seven days later.
4. From `ready_at_ms`, *anyone* calls `blast_partners::blast_partners::apply_share`
   or `blast_partners::blast_partners::apply_cap` with the shared `Partner`
   (mutable) and `Clock` at `0x6`; each emits `delayed::DelayedApplied`. Until a
   cap change is applied, the old cap keeps working, including for a thief
   holding it.
5. Instead, Blast may sign in and call `blast_partners::blast_partners::cancel_share`
   or `blast_partners::blast_partners::cancel_cap` (no `Clock`), even after
   readiness; each emits `delayed::DelayedCancelled`.

#### Pay a partner (products, or anyone)

`blast_partners::blast_partners::pay<T>` takes the shared `Partner` by immutable
reference, a `&mut Balance<T>` holding Blast's fee on a trade, and the
transaction context; it takes no `Clock`. It splits off `floor(fee * applied_share / 10,000)`, sends it to the partner's
`recipient`, emits `PartnerPaid<T>`, and returns the amount. The rest stays in
the caller's balance. With a zero share (or a fee too small to round above zero)
it sends nothing, emits nothing, and returns 0. Each positive payment creates
a `Coin<T>` and transfers it to the recipient with `public_transfer`. Integrators
never call `pay` directly; products call it inside their own partnered functions.

### How each product uses a partner

| Product | When the partner is named | Blast fee divided | Payment |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| Boosts | on each trade: `operations::mint_with_partner` or `operations::redeem_with_partner`, passing the `Partner` right after the `Protocol` | Blast's admin fee that trade retained; never the reserve fee | push: paid in the same call |
| Launchpad | once, at creation: `launch::set_partner` on the `LaunchInitializer` before `launch::finalize`; fixed for good | Blast's whole fee ledger on the launch: the creation fee (SUI), Blast's share of every curve trade fee (Meme and Quote), and Blast's one-time migration fee | held in the launch; paid when anyone calls `launch::claim_protocol_fees_with_partner` |
| LP vault (launchpad and presale) | inherited from the launch or sale at migration, in its vault terms | Blast's LP fees on each collection, and protocol migration residuals | held in the vault; paid when anyone calls `blast_vault::vault::pay_protocol_fees_with_partner` |
| Presale | once, at creation: `blast_presale::blast_presale::set_partner` on the `PresaleInitializer` before `blast_presale::blast_presale::finalize`; fixed for good | Blast's raise fee (Quote) only; Blast's token fee is never divided | held at migration; paid when anyone calls `blast_presale::blast_presale::pay_protocol_fees_with_partner` (once) |

Rules common to every product:

- Naming a partner requires that the product accepts it at that moment;
  otherwise the call aborts. Launches and sales that already name a removed
  partner keep paying it.
- The claim and payment calls are *anyone*: both payees are fixed, so no caller
  chooses where money goes. Each checks that the `Partner` passed is the one the
  launch, sale, or vault named, and aborts otherwise.
- A launch that names a partner cannot use `launch::claim_protocol_fees`; it
  must use `launch::claim_protocol_fees_with_partner`.
- Held fees are divided at the share **applied when they are paid**, not when
  they accrued.
- The `Partner` is read by immutable reference, so naming a busy partner adds no
  contention of its own.

### Economics

- Partner amount per payment: `floor(fee * share_bps / 10,000)` in atomic units
  of `T`, where `share_bps` is the applied share when the payment executes.
  Blast keeps the remainder, including every rounding dust.
- `share_bps` is between 0 and 8,000 (80%). Blast always keeps at least 20% of
  its fee.
- Users, holders, creators, buyers, and pools receive exactly the same amounts
  with or without a partner.
- Example (Boosts): a mint retains a 15.00 USDC admin fee and the partner's
  share is 4,000 BPS. The partner receives 6.00 USDC; Blast keeps 9.00 USDC.
- Example (presale): Blast's raise fee is 2,000 USDC and the share is 4,000 BPS.
  The partner receives 800 USDC; Blast keeps 1,200 USDC and all of its token
  fee.

### Time rules

- Delay: `blast_partners::blast_partners::delay_ms()` returns 604,800,000 (seven
  days). It is a compile-time constant; current upgrade authority is recorded in the deployment record.
- The initial share and managing cap apply at registration, without a Clock.
- A proposed share or cap can be applied by the first transaction whose `Clock`
  reads `ready_at_ms` or later; it takes effect only when `apply_share` or
  `apply_cap` succeeds. A replacement restarts the full seven days.
- A recipient change applies in the same transaction.
- A product removal keeps the partner accepted until `now + delay_ms()`; a trade
  or creation at or after that time aborts.

### Reading state

Move callers read a partner with `blast_partners::blast_partners::recipient`,
`blast_partners::blast_partners::share_bps` (applied),
`blast_partners::blast_partners::pending_share_bps`,
`blast_partners::blast_partners::cap_id` (applied), and
`blast_partners::blast_partners::pending_cap_id`; each pending reader returns an
`Option<delayed::Proposal<T>>`. `blast_partners::blast_partners::is_share_ready`
and `blast_partners::blast_partners::is_cap_ready` take the `Clock` and return
whether `apply_share` or `apply_cap` would succeed now: a proposal is pending,
the clock reads its `ready_at_ms` or later, and the clock plus the seven-day
notice still fits in a `u64`. They return false with nothing proposed, before
`ready_at_ms`, and after a cancel or an apply. The share readers keep the field's name,
`share_bps`, the repository's term for a partner's share in basis points.
`blast_partners::blast_partners::max_share_bps` returns the 8,000 BPS ceiling
`register` and `schedule_share` enforce. Off chain, read the shared `Partner`
object's content over RPC, or replay the events below.

- Applied share: `share_bps.value`.
- Pending share: `share_bps.pending`, with its `value` and `ready_at_ms`;
  readiness does not clear it.
- Which cap controls the recipient: `cap_id.value`; its proposal is
  `cap_id.pending`.
- Whether a product's `PartnerList` accepts a partner now:
  `blast_partners::partner_list::is_accepted`.
- Whether a product accepts a partner: read the product config's dynamic field
  `PartnerKey(partner_id)`, or the entry for `partner_id` in its embedded
  `PartnerList` table; accepted while it exists and `now < value`.
- Which partner a launch, sale, or vault names: its `partner_id: Option<ID>`
  field; a vault's comes from its terms, recorded in
  `blast_vault::blast_vault_events::VaultCreated`.

### Events

Every event is emitted directly (no envelope).

- `blast_partners::blast_partners::PartnerUpdated`: emitted by
  `blast_partners::blast_partners::register` and
  `blast_partners::blast_partners::set_recipient`. Fields: `partner_id`,
  `recipient`. The latest one per `partner_id` gives the recipient.
- Share and cap lifecycle events come from `blast_admin`'s `delayed` module,
  generic in the key (`ShareKey` for the share, `CapKey` for the cap) and the
  value type (`u64` or `ID`), with `parent` set to the `Partner`'s ID:
  `delayed::DelayedCreated` (registration: initial value and notice),
  `delayed::DelayedScheduled` (proposal: value, notice, `ready_at_ms`),
  `delayed::DelayedCancelled` (a cancel, or a proposal a new one replaced) and
  `delayed::DelayedApplied` (previous and new value, notice). Replaying them per
  `parent` and key reconstructs the applied and pending terms; `blast_partners`
  emits no event of its own for them.
- `blast_partners::partner_list::PartnerAdded`: emitted by
  `blast_partners::partner_list::add`. Fields: `parent` (the object embedding
  the list), `partner_id`.
- `blast_partners::partner_list::PartnerRemoved`: emitted by
  `blast_partners::partner_list::remove`. Fields: `parent`, `partner_id`,
  `acceptance_ends_at_ms` (exclusive). Replaying both per `parent` gives that
  list's accepted partners.
- `blast_partners::partner_list::PartnerListDestroyed`: emitted by
  `blast_partners::partner_list::destroy`. Field: `parent`. From then on the
  parent's list accepts no partner.
- `blast_partners::blast_partners::PartnerPaid<T>`: emitted by
  `blast_partners::blast_partners::pay` when it sends a positive amount. Fields:
  `partner_id`, `amount` (atomic units of `T`). The product records the trade in
  its own event in the same transaction:
  `blast_boosts_aftermath::events::PartnerFeePaid` (Boosts),
  `blast_core::blast_core_events::ProtocolFeesPaid` (launch claims, LP vaults and presale fees).
  `blast_fun::events::LaunchCreated` and
  `blast_presale::events::PresaleOpened` record the partner a launch or
  sale names.

### Aborts

| Code | Constant | Raised by | Fix |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| 0 | `EInvalidRecipient` | `register`, `set_recipient` | use a non-zero recipient |
| 1 | `EShareAboveMax` | `register`, `schedule_share` | share at most 8,000 BPS |
| 2 | `EUnchanged` | `schedule_share`, `schedule_cap` (value equals the pending proposal), `set_recipient` (same recipient) | pass a different value; to restart a pending proposal with the same value, cancel it first |
| 3 | `ENotLiveCap` | `set_recipient` | use the partner's applied cap; a ready but unapplied cap is refused until `apply_cap` |
| 4 | `EPartnerNotAccepted` | `partner_list::assert_accepted`, `partner_list::remove` | name a partner the list accepts now |

The codes come from the package registry `blast_partners::errors`. Codes 0 to 3
are raised in module `blast_partners`; `partner_list::add` and
`partner_list::remove` also raise `EUnchanged` (adding an accepted partner, or
removing one whose removal is pending), and code 4 is raised in module
`partner_list`. The share and cap calls also
abort in module `blast_admin::delayed` with `blast_admin`'s error codes:
`ENotReady` (13, `apply_share` or `apply_cap` before `ready_at_ms`),
`ENoPending` (14, apply or cancel with nothing proposed), `EInvalidDelay` (15, a
`ready_at_ms` that would overflow) and `EUnchanged` (16, proposing the applied
value). A product call that names a
partner it does not accept, or passes the wrong `Partner` to a claim, aborts in
that product's module with that product's own error.

### Pitfalls

- `PartnerPaid` alone does not prove a Blast trade:
  `blast_partners::blast_partners::pay` is public, and anyone can pay a partner
  from their own balance. Pair it with the product event in the same
  transaction.
- Each positive payment arrives as an owned `Coin<T>` at the recipient address.
  Repeated payouts produce separate coins that the recipient can merge.
- On Boosts, check acceptance before building a partnered trade; otherwise call
  the plain `mint` or `redeem`.
- On the launchpad and presales the partner is fixed at creation. Forgetting
  `launch::set_partner` or `blast_presale::blast_presale::set_partner` cannot be
  fixed later.
- Held fees are divided at the share applied at payment time. An applied cut
  to zero also cuts fees accrued earlier but paid later.
- A ready proposal is not in force. Payments keep the old share and the old cap
  keeps working until someone calls `apply_share` or `apply_cap`; a partner
  waiting on a raise can apply it itself.
- Whoever holds the cap controls the recipient of every deal under it. A stolen
  cap can redirect payments until a `schedule_cap` replacement is applied.

### Q&A

**How much can a partner earn?** Up to 80% of Blast's fee on the flow that names
it, as agreed with Blast and set by `blast_partners::blast_partners::register`.
Users pay the same fee with or without a partner.

**When am I paid?** On Boosts, in the same transaction as each mint or redeem.
On the launchpad and presales, when anyone calls the product's `*_with_partner`
claim or payment call; you may call it yourself.

**In which asset am I paid?** In the asset of the fee divided: Boost collateral;
launch Meme, Quote, and SUI; presale Quote; LP fees in both pool assets.

**Can Blast change my share without notice?** No. Every share or cap change
waits seven days, visible on chain the moment it is proposed, and only then can
anyone apply it. The notice is fixed in the published code.

**Can I change my recipient myself?** Yes, with your `PartnerCap` through
`blast_partners::blast_partners::set_recipient`; it applies at once.

**I lost my cap. What now?** Mint a new cap with
`blast_partners::blast_partners::new_cap` and ask Blast to call
`blast_partners::blast_partners::schedule_cap` for each of your partners. After
seven days anyone, you included, calls `blast_partners::blast_partners::apply_cap`;
until then the old cap still works.

**Can one company have several deals?** Yes. Each deal is its own `Partner` with
its own share and recipient; one `PartnerCap` can manage all of them.

**Can another app use my partner code?** Yes. The `Partner` is a public shared
object; any app can name it on a product that accepts it, and you earn on that
flow.

**How do I reconcile income?** Sum `PartnerPaid<T>` by `partner_id` and coin
type, and join each with the product event in the same transaction for trade
detail.

**Can I charge my own users an extra fee?** Not through `blast_partners`. Take
your own fee in your own PTB commands (split it from the user's coin and send it
where you like); Blast takes no cut of it.

## API reference: `blast_presale` (Presale)

Live on mainnet. This reference matches the published source.

Events: payload structs are emitted directly, each under its own type.

Abort codes: this package aborts with the numeric codes below from whichever module detects the failure; the abort location names that module.

| Code | Constant | Meaning |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 0 | `EUnguardedDelay` | Unpausing needs every policy delay nonzero, with no pending zero delay or admission. |
| 1 | `EAllowanceUnchanged` | An allowlist update must change whether the type or partner is allowed. |
| 2 | `EQuoteNotAllowed` | This Quote type is not allowed for new presales. |
| 3 | `EVenueNotAllowed` | This venue is not allowed for new presales. |
| 4 | `EInvalidVenue` | A venue must be a non-framework struct, never a primitive, vector, or framework type any caller can construct. |
| 5 | `EInvalidFees` | Blast's raise and token shares must be at most 1,000 BPS and each LP fee at most 3,000. |
| 6 | `EInvalidAssetPair` | The sale token and Quote type must differ. |
| 7 | `EInvalidRaiseTarget` | The raise target must be positive. |
| 8 | `EInvalidTokenAllocation` | The supply must be positive, and token allocations unique, positive, bounded, totalling exactly 10,000 BPS with Blast's share, and apportioning to positive amounts. |
| 9 | `EInvalidWindow` | The commitment window must be positive, the liquidity window at least seven days, and both must end within u64 milliseconds. |
| 10 | `EInvalidEligibilityPolicy` | The eligibility policy has an invalid cap, participant bound, root, or leaf count. |
| 11 | `EInvalidLifecycle` | The presale is not in the lifecycle required by this operation. |
| 12 | `EPresaleEnded` | The presale commitment window has ended. |
| 13 | `EPresaleNotEnded` | The presale commitment window has not ended, and the sale cannot close early: its raise is not full under a hard cap that closes it when filled. |
| 14 | `EZeroCommitment` | A presale commitment must be positive. |
| 15 | `EWalletCapExceeded` | The wallet commitment exceeds its eligibility cap. |
| 16 | `EParticipantCapacityReached` | The presale already contains its maximum number of participants. |
| 17 | `EInvalidEligibilityProof` | The submitted wallet, cap, index, or proof does not match the allowlist. |
| 18 | `EWrongEligibilityPolicy` | The commit function does not match the presale eligibility policy. |
| 19 | `ETotalCommitmentOverflow` | Adding the commitment would overflow the presale total. |
| 20 | `ENoCommitment` | The wallet has no undelivered presale commitment. |
| 21 | `EWrongPresaleCap` | The creator capability belongs to a different presale. |
| 22 | `ELiquidityDeadlineNotReached` | The deadline to cancel an unlaunched funded presale has not arrived. |
| 23 | `EInitialSupplyNotZero` | The supplied SaleToken TreasuryCap must have zero total supply. |
| 24 | `EBelowMinimumCommitment` | The wallet's total commitment is below the sale's minimum commitment. |
| 25 | `EUnreachableRaise` | The minimum commitment times the slots held to it, the participant bound of an open policy or the open-to-anyone phase slots beyond any allowlist, must reach the raise target. |
| 26 | `EUnseedablePrice` | The raise and LP-token allocation give a seed price the venues cannot pool. |
| 27 | `EInvalidUncappedPhase` | An uncapped phase must start inside the window, and an open-to-anyone phase needs a positive minimum and a participant bound between the policy's and 65,536. |
| 28 | `EUncappedPhaseNotOpen` | The presale has no uncapped phase open to any wallet, or it has not started. |
| 29 | `EPresaleFilled` | The presale has already reached its raise under its hard cap. |
| 30 | `EInvalidRaiseAllocation` | Raise allocations must be unique, positive, bounded, leave the LP a share after Blast's, and apportion to positive amounts. |
| 31 | `EPresaleNotStarted` | The presale commitment window has not started. |
| 32 | `EPresaleStarted` | A presale's Blast rates can change only before its commitment window starts. |
| 33 | `EProtocolFeesUnchanged` | A presale fee update must change its stored rates. |
| 34 | `EInvalidHardCap` | A hard cap must start inside the commitment window. |
| 35 | `EMissingSetting` | A presale needs its eligibility policy and LP-fee split set before it is finalized. |
| 36 | `ESaleTokenNotUnregulated` | The sale currency must be confirmed unregulated; regulated and unknown legacy currencies are not allowed. |
| 37 | `EAboveFeePolicy` | A sale fee override cannot exceed the currently applied fee policy. |
| 38 | `EProtocolFeesIncreased` | A shared sale's fee override may only keep or lower each of its rates. |

### `blast_presale::blast_presale`

Standalone presales with capped commitments, proportional fills or a first-come hard cap, migration-gated claims, full refunds when a sale does not migrate, the migration hand-off to the sale's venue, and the owner proof its vault accepts.

Source: https://github.com/interest-protocol/blast-v2-contracts/blob/main/sui/launch/presale/sources/blast_presale.move

#### Types

```move
/// Shared singleton every presale's object ID derives from, so a client finds a coin's one sale
/// from the coin type alone.
public struct PresaleRegistry has key {
    id: UID,
}

/// Derives the one presale of `SaleToken` from the registry.
public struct PresaleKey<phantom SaleToken>() has copy, drop, store;

/// Who may commit and the maximum Quote each wallet may commit.
///
/// `Open` gives every wallet the same cap and minimum. The minimum times the
/// participant bound must reach the raise, so filling every slot funds the sale
/// and dust commitments cannot lock real buyers out. `Allowlisted` commits one
/// cap per wallet in a positional Blake2b-256 Merkle tree, supporting equal,
/// tiered, or fully custom caps.
public enum EligibilityPolicy has copy, drop, store {
    Open {
        wallet_cap: u64,
        max_participants: u64,
        min_commitment: u64,
    },
    Allowlisted {
        root: vector<u8>,
        leaf_count: u64,
    },
}

/// Who may commit once an uncapped phase lifts wallet caps.
public enum UncappedAccess has copy, drop, store {
    /// Only wallets the sale's eligibility policy admits, without their caps.
    Eligible,
    /// Any wallet, bounded like an open policy: `min_commitment` over the slots held to it must
    /// reach the raise so dust cannot lock buyers out. Every slot counts under an open policy;
    /// under an allowlist only the slots beyond its leaves do, since allowlisted wallets may
    /// commit any positive amount.
    Anyone {
        max_participants: u64,
        min_commitment: u64,
    },
}

/// An optional closing phase, starting `starts_after_ms` into the commitment window,
/// that lifts wallet caps. Whether the sale then overflows or stops at its raise is the
/// sale's `HardCap`, not the phase's.
public struct UncappedPhase has copy, drop, store {
    starts_after_ms: u64,
    access: UncappedAccess,
}

/// From `starts_after_ms` into the commitment window, the sale takes Quote only up to its
/// raise, first come, first served: a commitment takes what is left and returns the excess at
/// once, and the sale stops accepting Quote when the raise is full. Before then it overflows,
/// filling every wallet at one common rate. With `closes_when_filled`, a sale whose raise is full
/// under its hard cap may settle at once instead of waiting for the window's end.
public struct HardCap has copy, drop, store {
    starts_after_ms: u64,
    closes_when_filled: bool,
}

/// The presale's one-way lifecycle.
public enum Lifecycle has copy, drop, store {
    Pending,
    Open,
    Funded,
    Migrated,
    Cancelled,
}

/// One creator's authority over one presale.
public struct PresaleCap has key, store {
    id: UID,
    presale_id: ID,
}

/// One recipient's share, in BPS, of the token supply or of the raise, paid at migration or
/// vested from it as its `blast_core::payout::Recipient` says.
public struct RecipientShare has copy, drop, store {
    recipient: blast_core::payout::Recipient,
    bps: blast_core::bps::Bps,
}

/// The exact token or Quote amount one recipient receives at migration, apportioned from its
/// `RecipientShare` when the presale is created.
public struct RecipientPayout has copy, drop, store {
    recipient: blast_core::payout::Recipient,
    amount: u64,
}

/// Dynamic-field name for one wallet's undelivered commitment.
public struct CommitmentKey(address) has copy, drop, store;

/// One wallet's Quote commitment and immutable cap.
public struct Commitment has copy, drop, store {
    quote: u64,
    wallet_cap: u64,
}

/// Hot potato from `new` to `finalize`, collecting the creator's settings. Only `finalize`
/// consumes it, so every sale is finalized in the transaction whose `new` checked the creation
/// policy and snapshotted Blast's rates. `finalize` validates the settings together; the supply,
/// token allocations, raise, windows, eligibility policy, and the vault's LP-fee split must all be
/// set.
public struct PresaleInitializer<phantom SaleToken, phantom Quote> {
    treasury_cap: TreasuryCap<SaleToken>,
    venue: TypeName,
    protocol_fees: fee_policy::ProtocolFees,
    token_supply: u64,
    burnable: bool,
    token_allocation_bps: blast_core::bps::Bps,
    liquidity_token_allocation_bps: blast_core::bps::Bps,
    recipient_allocations: vector<RecipientShare>,
    participant_vesting: Option<blast_core::payout::VestingTerms>,
    raise_target: u64,
    raise_allocations: vector<RecipientShare>,
    duration_ms: u64,
    liquidity_window_ms: u64,
    eligibility: Option<EligibilityPolicy>,
    uncapped: Option<UncappedPhase>,
    hard_cap: Option<HardCap>,
    fee_split: Option<blast_core::fee_split::FeeSplit>,
    partner_id: Option<ID>,
}

/// One standalone presale and its complete custody until migration or refund.
///
/// The complete fixed or burn-only token supply is apportioned at creation.
/// Presale, LP, Blast, and additional recipient allocations stay escrowed until
/// a funded migration atomically releases them. Blast's rates are snapshotted
/// at creation, Blast's `FeeAdmin` may change them until the window starts, and its
/// fees move into the sale's protocol-fee ledger on migration, which pays them to the treasury at
/// once, or, when the sale names a Blast partner, holds them until
/// `pay_protocol_fees_with_partner` pays the partner its cut of each and the treasury the rest.
/// If the raise is missed, every commitment
/// is refunded. Anyone may migrate a funded sale until it is cancelled; once LP
/// is still unmigrated at its deadline, anyone may cancel it so every
/// commitment becomes refundable. Under participant vesting, each claim locks the
/// participant's tokens in its own schedule instead of paying them out.
public struct Presale<phantom SaleToken, phantom Quote> has key {
    id: UID,
    presale_cap_id: ID,
    lifecycle: Lifecycle,
    /// The commitment window's length: as created until the sale closes early, then the time it
    /// ran.
    duration_ms: u64,
    liquidity_window_ms: u64,
    /// When commitments open, fixed by `share`; zero until then. The window's end, the LP
    /// deadline, and the uncapped and hard-cap starts are derived from it and the lengths above.
    start_ms: u64,
    raise_target: u64,
    eligibility: EligibilityPolicy,
    uncapped: Option<UncappedPhase>,
    hard_cap: Option<HardCap>,
    /// The venue witness type the sale's liquidity migrates to; only it redeems the hand-off.
    venue: TypeName,
    /// The LP-fee split the sale's vault starts with.
    fee_split: blast_core::fee_split::FeeSplit,
    total_commitments: u64,
    /// The wallets holding an undelivered commitment: admitted by their first commitment and
    /// released by their claim, so the claim that empties the sale is the last one.
    participants: u64,
    quote: Balance<Quote>,
    token_allocation: u64,
    participant_vesting: Option<blast_core::payout::VestingTerms>,
    sale_tokens: Balance<SaleToken>,
    liquidity_tokens: Balance<SaleToken>,
    recipient_payouts: vector<RecipientPayout>,
    recipient_tokens: Balance<SaleToken>,
    raise_payouts: vector<RecipientPayout>,
    protocol_fees: fee_policy::ProtocolFees,
    protocol_quote: u64,
    protocol_tokens: Balance<SaleToken>,
    /// The Blast partner named at creation, paid its cut of Blast's fees. It never changes once
    /// the sale is shared.
    partner_id: Option<ID>,
    /// Blast's raise and token fees from migration until paid; bound to `partner_id`.
    fee_ledger: blast_core::protocol_fees::Ledger<SaleToken, Quote>,
}
```

#### Functions

```move
/// Creates one equal-cap policy with a bounded number of distinct wallets, each
/// of which must commit at least `min_commitment` in total.
public fun open_eligibility(
    wallet_cap: u64,
    max_participants: u64,
    min_commitment: u64,
): EligibilityPolicy

/// Creates one allowlist policy whose leaves are `(index, wallet, wallet_cap)`.
public fun allowlisted_eligibility(root: vector<u8>, leaf_count: u64): EligibilityPolicy

/// Lifts wallet caps for eligible wallets `start_delay_ms` into the window.
public fun uncapped_for_eligible(start_delay_ms: u64): UncappedPhase

/// Opens the sale to any wallet without caps `start_delay_ms` into the window, admitting
/// at most `max_participants` wallets in total, each committing at least `min_commitment`.
public fun uncapped_for_anyone(
    start_delay_ms: u64,
    max_participants: u64,
    min_commitment: u64,
): UncappedPhase

/// One recipient's share in BPS of the supply or the raise, paid or vested at migration as
/// `recipient` says. Presale creation validates each list of shares under that list's own rules
/// and error.
public fun recipient_share(
    recipient: blast_core::payout::Recipient,
    bps: blast_core::bps::Bps,
): RecipientShare

/// Starts a presale of `SaleToken` raising `Quote` whose liquidity migrates to the venue that
/// issued `venue_ticket` for the pair, and snapshots Blast's rates. The venue keeps the pool terms,
/// such as a CLMM tier, which it records while preparing the ticket.
///
/// `sealed_treasury_cap` is the cap `sale_token::new` sealed at the coin's publication; only this
/// call opens it, so the creator never mints, and a venue minted nothing while preparing.
///
/// A venue in this or any other package issues tickets sealed by its own witness, which only its
/// defining module can construct. The `CreationPolicy` must admit that venue, so Blast decides
/// which venues may open sales; the sale's migration hand-off is then redeemable only by it.
public fun new<SaleToken, Quote>(
    sealed_treasury_cap: blast_core::sealed_treasury_cap::SealedTreasuryCap<SaleToken>,
    package_version: &blast_admin::package_version::PackageVersion<blast_presale_package::BLAST_PRESALE_PACKAGE>,
    venue_ticket: blast_core::migration::VenueTicket<SaleToken, Quote>,
    creation_policy: &creation_policy::CreationPolicy,
    fee_policy: &fee_policy::FeePolicy,
): PresaleInitializer<SaleToken, Quote>

/// Sets the complete supply `finalize` mints, and whether it then becomes burn-only rather than
/// fixed.
public fun set_supply<SaleToken, Quote>(
    self: &mut PresaleInitializer<SaleToken, Quote>,
    package_version: &blast_admin::package_version::PackageVersion<blast_presale_package::BLAST_PRESALE_PACKAGE>,
    token_supply: u64,
    burnable: bool,
)

/// Splits the supply in BPS between the buyers, the LP, and each recipient. With Blast's token
/// rate the shares must total exactly 10,000 BPS.
public fun set_token_allocations<SaleToken, Quote>(
    self: &mut PresaleInitializer<SaleToken, Quote>,
    package_version: &blast_admin::package_version::PackageVersion<blast_presale_package::BLAST_PRESALE_PACKAGE>,
    token_allocation_bps: blast_core::bps::Bps,
    liquidity_token_allocation_bps: blast_core::bps::Bps,
    recipient_allocations: vector<RecipientShare>,
)

/// Locks each participant's claimed tokens in its own schedule under `vesting_terms`, starting at
/// its claim, instead of paying them out. Without it, claims pay tokens out at once.
public fun set_participant_vesting<SaleToken, Quote>(
    self: &mut PresaleInitializer<SaleToken, Quote>,
    package_version: &blast_admin::package_version::PackageVersion<blast_presale_package::BLAST_PRESALE_PACKAGE>,
    vesting_terms: blast_core::payout::VestingTerms,
)

/// Sets the Quote the sale raises and each recipient's share of it, paid at migration. The LP
/// keeps what Blast's and the recipients' shares leave.
public fun set_raise<SaleToken, Quote>(
    self: &mut PresaleInitializer<SaleToken, Quote>,
    package_version: &blast_admin::package_version::PackageVersion<blast_presale_package::BLAST_PRESALE_PACKAGE>,
    raise_target: u64,
    raise_allocations: vector<RecipientShare>,
)

/// Sets how long commitments stay open from the start, and how long after the window anyone may
/// migrate a funded sale before anyone may cancel it: at least seven days.
public fun set_windows<SaleToken, Quote>(
    self: &mut PresaleInitializer<SaleToken, Quote>,
    package_version: &blast_admin::package_version::PackageVersion<blast_presale_package::BLAST_PRESALE_PACKAGE>,
    commitment_duration_ms: u64,
    liquidity_window_duration_ms: u64,
)

/// Sets who may commit and each wallet's cap.
public fun set_eligibility<SaleToken, Quote>(
    self: &mut PresaleInitializer<SaleToken, Quote>,
    package_version: &blast_admin::package_version::PackageVersion<blast_presale_package::BLAST_PRESALE_PACKAGE>,
    eligibility: EligibilityPolicy,
)

/// Adds a closing phase that lifts wallet caps. Without one, caps hold for the whole window.
public fun set_uncapped_phase<SaleToken, Quote>(
    self: &mut PresaleInitializer<SaleToken, Quote>,
    package_version: &blast_admin::package_version::PackageVersion<blast_presale_package::BLAST_PRESALE_PACKAGE>,
    uncapped: UncappedPhase,
)

/// Hard-caps the sale at its raise from `start_delay_ms` into the window: zero for the whole
/// window, or the uncapped phase's offset to keep an overflowing capped phase before it. With
/// `closes_when_filled`, anyone may settle the sale once its raise is full under the hard cap;
/// without it, a full sale waits for the window's end. Without a hard cap, the sale overflows
/// for the whole window and always runs to its end.
public fun set_hard_cap<SaleToken, Quote>(
    self: &mut PresaleInitializer<SaleToken, Quote>,
    package_version: &blast_admin::package_version::PackageVersion<blast_presale_package::BLAST_PRESALE_PACKAGE>,
    start_delay_ms: u64,
    closes_when_filled: bool,
)

/// Sets how the sale's vault splits every collected LP fee after Blast's share: any of the core
/// routes, including the sale owner, auto-LP, holder rewards, and burn.
public fun set_fee_split<SaleToken, Quote>(
    self: &mut PresaleInitializer<SaleToken, Quote>,
    package_version: &blast_admin::package_version::PackageVersion<blast_presale_package::BLAST_PRESALE_PACKAGE>,
    fee_split: blast_core::fee_split::FeeSplit,
)

/// Names the sale's Blast partner, one the `CreationPolicy` lets new presales name; from
/// `finalize` on it is the sale's partner for good. The partner earns its cut of Blast's raise and
/// token fees, which migration holds until `pay_protocol_fees_with_partner` pays them, and its
/// vault inherits the partner, which then earns its cut of Blast's LP fees (ADR 0137).
public fun set_partner<SaleToken, Quote>(
    self: &mut PresaleInitializer<SaleToken, Quote>,
    package_version: &blast_admin::package_version::PackageVersion<blast_presale_package::BLAST_PRESALE_PACKAGE>,
    creation_policy: &creation_policy::CreationPolicy,
    partner: &blast_partners::blast_partners::Partner,
    clock: &Clock,
)

/// The venue witness type the sale migrates to, so a venue finalizing an initializer it is
/// handed can confirm the sale is its own.
public fun venue<SaleToken, Quote>(self: &PresaleInitializer<SaleToken, Quote>): TypeName

/// Requires a confirmed unregulated sale currency and checks every setting, then mints and
/// apportions the complete token supply and
/// irreversibly makes it fixed or burn-only before returning the presale for `share`.
public fun finalize<SaleToken, Quote>(
    self: PresaleInitializer<SaleToken, Quote>,
    package_version: &blast_admin::package_version::PackageVersion<blast_presale_package::BLAST_PRESALE_PACKAGE>,
    registry: &mut PresaleRegistry,
    currency: &mut Currency<SaleToken>,
    ctx: &mut TxContext,
): (Presale<SaleToken, Quote>, PresaleCap)

/// Shares a newly created presale and fixes its windows from `commitment_starts_at_ms`, or from now once that
/// has passed, so a sale can be created ahead of its start. Commitments open at the start; until
/// then Blast's `FeeAdmin` may change the sale's Blast rates.
public fun share<SaleToken, Quote>(
    self: Presale<SaleToken, Quote>,
    package_version: &blast_admin::package_version::PackageVersion<blast_presale_package::BLAST_PRESALE_PACKAGE>,
    commitment_starts_at_ms: u64,
    clock: &Clock,
    _: &mut TxContext,
)

/// Replaces this sale's snapshot of Blast's rates before its window starts, while nobody can have
/// committed: in its creation transaction before `share`, or once shared until its start. Once
/// shared the sale is public, so each rate may only stay or fall; only the creation transaction
/// may raise one, up to the applied fee policy. A changed token rate reapportions Blast's tokens
/// against the buyers' sale allocation and a changed raise rate its Quote against the LP's, each
/// rounded down; an unchanged rate keeps its amount, and every recipient keeps its amount.
public fun set_protocol_fees<SaleToken, Quote>(
    self: &mut Presale<SaleToken, Quote>,
    package_version: &blast_admin::package_version::PackageVersion<blast_presale_package::BLAST_PRESALE_PACKAGE>,
    _: &blast_admin::blast_acl::AdminWitness<blast_core::blast_domain::FeeAdmin>,
    fee_policy: &fee_policy::FeePolicy,
    raise_bps: blast_core::bps::Bps,
    token_bps: blast_core::bps::Bps,
    lp_fee_meme_bps: blast_core::bps::Bps,
    lp_fee_quote_bps: blast_core::bps::Bps,
    clock: &Clock,
)

/// Locks the supplied `Coin<Quote>` in this presale under an equal-cap, open
/// eligibility policy and returns whatever a hard-capped uncapped phase could not
/// accept (a zero coin otherwise).
public fun commit_open<SaleToken, Quote>(
    self: &mut Presale<SaleToken, Quote>,
    package_version: &blast_admin::package_version::PackageVersion<blast_presale_package::BLAST_PRESALE_PACKAGE>,
    quote_in: Coin<Quote>,
    clock: &Clock,
    ctx: &mut TxContext,
): Coin<Quote>

/// Locks Quote from an allowlisted wallet after verifying its Merkle-committed cap,
/// returning whatever a hard cap could not accept.
public fun commit_allowlisted<SaleToken, Quote>(
    self: &mut Presale<SaleToken, Quote>,
    package_version: &blast_admin::package_version::PackageVersion<blast_presale_package::BLAST_PRESALE_PACKAGE>,
    quote_in: Coin<Quote>,
    index: u64,
    wallet_cap: u64,
    proof: vector<vector<u8>>,
    clock: &Clock,
    ctx: &mut TxContext,
): Coin<Quote>

/// Locks Quote from any wallet once an uncapped phase open to anyone has started,
/// returning whatever a hard cap could not accept.
public fun commit_uncapped<SaleToken, Quote>(
    self: &mut Presale<SaleToken, Quote>,
    package_version: &blast_admin::package_version::PackageVersion<blast_presale_package::BLAST_PRESALE_PACKAGE>,
    quote_in: Coin<Quote>,
    clock: &Clock,
    ctx: &mut TxContext,
): Coin<Quote>

/// Fixes the fill after the commitment window, or as soon as the raise is full under a hard cap
/// that closes the sale when filled; closing early ends the window now, so the LP deadline counts
/// from now. Underfunded sales become fully refundable. Anyone may settle.
public fun settle<SaleToken, Quote>(
    self: &mut Presale<SaleToken, Quote>,
    package_version: &blast_admin::package_version::PackageVersion<blast_presale_package::BLAST_PRESALE_PACKAGE>,
    clock: &Clock,
    _: &mut TxContext,
)

/// Cancels a funded presale still unmigrated at its LP deadline, preserving all
/// Quote for refunds. Anyone may cancel.
public fun cancel_unlaunched<SaleToken, Quote>(
    self: &mut Presale<SaleToken, Quote>,
    package_version: &blast_admin::package_version::PackageVersion<blast_presale_package::BLAST_PRESALE_PACKAGE>,
    clock: &Clock,
    _: &mut TxContext,
)

/// Delivers one participant's pro-rata tokens and overflow Quote after migration,
/// or its full Quote after cancellation. Under participant vesting, the tokens are
/// locked in a shared irrevocable linear schedule starting at this claim instead of
/// paid out; the Quote refund is always paid at once. Anyone may relay; the final
/// claim receives the bounded integer-rounding remainder.
public fun claim<SaleToken, Quote>(
    self: &mut Presale<SaleToken, Quote>,
    package_version: &blast_admin::package_version::PackageVersion<blast_presale_package::BLAST_PRESALE_PACKAGE>,
    participant: address,
    clock: &Clock,
    ctx: &mut TxContext,
)

/// Pays Blast's raise and token fees to `treasury` and the raise recipients their shares, and
/// releases every token recipient's tokens at once, locking each vesting share in its own shared
/// irrevocable schedule starting now, then returns the hand-off only the stored venue can redeem:
/// the rest of the raise, the LP-token allocation, and the vault's terms, with the sale's LP-fee
/// split, Blast's snapshotted LP-fee rates, its partner, and the migration residuals kept for the
/// owner. A sale naming a Blast partner holds Blast's fees in its ledger instead, for
/// `pay_protocol_fees_with_partner`. Anyone may call it until the sale is cancelled; an unredeemed
/// hand-off aborts the transaction, so the venue's pool creation stays atomic with this state
/// transition.
public fun migrate<SaleToken, Quote>(
    self: &mut Presale<SaleToken, Quote>,
    package_version: &blast_admin::package_version::PackageVersion<blast_presale_package::BLAST_PRESALE_PACKAGE>,
    treasury: &blast_core::blast_treasury::Treasury,
    clock: &Clock,
    ctx: &mut TxContext,
): blast_core::migration::Migration<SaleToken, Quote>

/// Proves to the sale's vault that the caller holds the sale's creator capability.
public fun prove_owner<SaleToken, Quote>(
    self: &Presale<SaleToken, Quote>,
    package_version: &blast_admin::package_version::PackageVersion<blast_presale_package::BLAST_PRESALE_PACKAGE>,
    cap: &PresaleCap,
): blast_core::owner_proof::OwnerProof

/// Pays a migrated sale's Blast partner its cut of each of Blast's fees the migration held, rounded
/// down so Blast keeps the remainder, and `treasury` the rest. The cut is the partner's
/// `blast_partners` applied rate. Anyone may call it: both recipients are fixed, and a ledger with
/// nothing held, before migration or once paid, sends nothing.
public fun pay_protocol_fees_with_partner<SaleToken, Quote>(
    self: &mut Presale<SaleToken, Quote>,
    package_version: &blast_admin::package_version::PackageVersion<blast_presale_package::BLAST_PRESALE_PACKAGE>,
    treasury: &blast_core::blast_treasury::Treasury,
    partner: &blast_partners::blast_partners::Partner,
    ctx: &mut TxContext,
)

/// Destroys the creator capability after cancellation. All minted SaleToken
/// allocations remain permanently locked in the cancelled presale.
public fun destroy_cap<SaleToken, Quote>(self: &Presale<SaleToken, Quote>, cap: PresaleCap)
```

```move
/// A presale was created with fully funded token allocations.
public struct PresaleCreated<phantom SaleToken, phantom Quote> has copy, drop {
    presale_id: ID,
    presale_cap_id: ID,
    treasury_cap_id: ID,
    token_supply: u64,
    token_allocation_bps: u64,
    participant_vesting: Option<blast_core::payout::VestingTerms>,
    liquidity_token_allocation_bps: u64,
    /// Each token recipient, with the terms it vests under, if any, and its share.
    recipients: vector<blast_core::payout::Recipient>,
    recipient_bps: vector<u64>,
    burnable: bool,
    raise_target: u64,
    /// Each raise recipient, with the terms it vests under, if any, and its share.
    raise_recipients: vector<blast_core::payout::Recipient>,
    raise_recipient_bps: vector<u64>,
    duration_ms: u64,
    liquidity_window_ms: u64,
    eligibility: EligibilityPolicy,

    protocol_raise_bps: u64,
    protocol_token_bps: u64,
    protocol_lp_fee_meme_bps: u64,
    protocol_lp_fee_quote_bps: u64,
    /// Blast's shares of the raise and of the supply, paid on migration.
    protocol_quote: u64,
    protocol_tokens: u64,
}
```

### `blast_presale::blast_presale_package`

The presale's publication and the one constructor of its package witness.  Publication hands the deployer an owned `Publication<BLAST_PRESALE_PACKAGE>`, which `blast_admin::package_admin::wrap` turns into the shared `PackageAdmin<BLAST_PRESALE_PACKAGE>` and the shared `PackageVersion<BLAST_PRESALE_PACKAGE>`, fully paused. Pausing, upgrades, and immutability go through `blast_admin::package_admin` under the package-scoped roles `PauseAdmin<BLAST_PRESALE_PACKAGE>`, `UpgradeAdmin<BLAST_PRESALE_PACKAGE>`, and `CancelAdmin<BLAST_PRESALE_PACKAGE>`.  The fee and creation policies start with zero delays so Blast configures them while the package is paused; `creation_policy::unpause` refuses until every delay is guarded, then clears bits with the package witness. It lives beside the policies it reads because they name `BLAST_PRESALE_PACKAGE`, so this module cannot import them.

Source: https://github.com/interest-protocol/blast-v2-contracts/blob/main/sui/launch/presale/sources/blast_presale_package.move

#### Types

```move
/// The package's one-time witness (OTW), typing its version, publication, and roles.
public struct BLAST_PRESALE_PACKAGE() has drop;

/// The presale's one package witness, pinned at publication. It seals each sale's protocol-fee
/// ledger, migration, vault terms and owner proofs, and clears pause bits through the generic
/// administration. Only this module constructs it, through the package-only `package_witness`;
/// nothing stores it or returns it outside the package.
public struct PackageWitness() has drop;
```

### `blast_presale::constants`

Package-wide presale constants exposed as zero-cost macros. The basis-points denominator and Blast's LP-fee share maximum come from `blast_core::constants`.

Source: https://github.com/interest-protocol/blast-v2-contracts/blob/main/sui/launch/presale/sources/lib/constants.move

#### Constants

- `version: u64 = 1`: The package version this bytecode serves.
- `create_pause_bit: u8 = 1`: Stops presale creation and sharing.
- `commit_pause_bit: u8 = 2`: Stops open, allowlisted, and uncapped commitments.
- `migration_pause_bit: u8 = 4`: Stops LP migration.
- `valid_pause_bits: u8 = blast_presale::constants::create_pause_bit!() | blast_presale::constants::commit_pause_bit!() | blast_presale::constants::migration_pause_bit!()`: Every supported presale pause flag.
- `max_participants: u64 = 65_536`: Technical bound on the dynamic commitment records one presale may hold.
- `max_recipients: u64 = 64`: Bounds the token and raise recipient lists, both paid in the one migration transaction.
- `min_liquidity_window_duration_ms: u64 = 7 * 24 * 60 * 60 * 1_000`: The shortest LP-migration window. Migration is permissionless, so a funded sale reaches its refund exit only after a week in which nobody migrated it, and a sale that nobody settled by its end cannot be settled and cancelled in one transaction inside that week.
- `max_raise_bps: u64 = 1_000`: Blast's largest share of a sale's raise.
- `max_token_bps: u64 = 1_000`: Blast's largest share of a sale's tokens.
- `digest_length: u64 = 32`: Byte length of a Blake2b-256 digest: an allowlist's Merkle root.
- `allowlist_leaf_domain: vector<u8> = b"blast-presale-v1"`: The Merkle leaf domain of every presale allowlist, `blast_core::merkle::leaf`'s domain for each `(index, wallet, wallet_cap)` payload.

### `blast_presale::creation_policy`

Blast's allowlists for new presales: the Quote coins a sale may raise, the venues whose tickets may start a sale, and the Blast partners a sale may name; and the presale's `unpause`, which reads these and the fee policy's delays.  A presale is checked once, when it is created. Later changes apply only to later sales, so a delisting never strands a live sale's commitments, blocks its migration, or stops paying its partner.

Source: https://github.com/interest-protocol/blast-v2-contracts/blob/main/sui/launch/presale/sources/config/creation_policy.move

#### Types

```move
/// Shared singleton holding the Quote types, venues, and partners new presales may use.
public struct CreationPolicy has key {
    id: UID,
    allowed_quotes: VecSet<TypeName>,
    venues: blast_admin::delayed_set::DelayedSet<VenueKey, TypeName>,
    partners: blast_partners::partner_list::PartnerList,
}

/// Keys the delayed lifecycle of venue admissions and their delay.
public struct VenueKey() has copy, drop, store;
```

#### Functions

```move
/// Allows or disallows `Quote` for presales created afterward; existing ones keep it.
public fun set_quote_allowed<Quote>(
    self: &mut CreationPolicy,
    package_version: &blast_admin::package_version::PackageVersion<blast_presale_package::BLAST_PRESALE_PACKAGE>,
    _: &blast_admin::blast_acl::AdminWitness<blast_core::blast_domain::ListingAdmin>,
    allowed: bool,
)

/// Admits a venue's witness type after the applied admission delay. An admitted venue's tickets
/// start presales afterward, and only that venue redeems their migrations; existing sales keep
/// their venue. Rescheduling a pending admission restarts its delay.
///
/// `presale::new` and `presale::migrate` are public, so whoever can construct an admitted witness
/// can open sales and redeem their liquidity. Admit only a struct whose defining module is the
/// reviewed venue and keeps its constructor private. A primitive, a vector, or a framework type,
/// any of which a caller can construct, is rejected.
public fun schedule_venue<Venue: drop>(
    self: &mut CreationPolicy,
    package_version: &blast_admin::package_version::PackageVersion<blast_presale_package::BLAST_PRESALE_PACKAGE>,
    _: &blast_admin::blast_acl::AdminWitness<blast_core::blast_domain::VenueAdmin>,
    clock: &Clock,
)

/// Permissionlessly applies the approved admission once ready.
public fun apply_venue<Venue: drop>(
    self: &mut CreationPolicy,
    package_version: &blast_admin::package_version::PackageVersion<blast_presale_package::BLAST_PRESALE_PACKAGE>,
    clock: &Clock,
)

/// Cancels a pending venue admission, including after its threshold.
public fun cancel_venue<Venue: drop>(
    self: &mut CreationPolicy,
    package_version: &blast_admin::package_version::PackageVersion<blast_presale_package::BLAST_PRESALE_PACKAGE>,
    _: &blast_admin::blast_acl::AdminWitness<blast_core::blast_domain::CancelAdmin>,
)

/// Immediately removes a venue for new presales and cancels any pending admission.
public fun remove_venue<Venue: drop>(
    self: &mut CreationPolicy,
    package_version: &blast_admin::package_version::PackageVersion<blast_presale_package::BLAST_PRESALE_PACKAGE>,
    _: &blast_admin::blast_acl::AdminWitness<blast_core::blast_domain::VenueAdmin>,
)

/// Lets new presales name `partner`, at once. Adding a partner whose removal is pending cancels
/// the removal; adding a removed partner accepts it again.
public fun add_partner(
    self: &mut CreationPolicy,
    package_version: &blast_admin::package_version::PackageVersion<blast_presale_package::BLAST_PRESALE_PACKAGE>,
    _: &blast_admin::blast_acl::AdminWitness<blast_core::blast_domain::FeeAdmin>,
    partner: &blast_partners::blast_partners::Partner,
)

/// Stops new presales naming `partner` after the delay Blast gives for every change to a
/// partner, `blast_partners::delay_ms()`. Sales that already name it keep paying it.
public fun remove_partner(
    self: &mut CreationPolicy,
    package_version: &blast_admin::package_version::PackageVersion<blast_presale_package::BLAST_PRESALE_PACKAGE>,
    _: &blast_admin::blast_acl::AdminWitness<blast_core::blast_domain::FeeAdmin>,
    partner: &blast_partners::blast_partners::Partner,
    clock: &Clock,
)

/// Proposes only the admission delay. Existing admission deadlines remain fixed.
public fun schedule_venue_delay(
    self: &mut CreationPolicy,
    package_version: &blast_admin::package_version::PackageVersion<blast_presale_package::BLAST_PRESALE_PACKAGE>,
    _: &blast_admin::blast_acl::AdminWitness<blast_core::blast_domain::VenueAdmin>,
    delay_ms: u64,
    clock: &Clock,
)

/// Permissionlessly applies a ready admission-delay proposal.
public fun apply_venue_delay(
    self: &mut CreationPolicy,
    package_version: &blast_admin::package_version::PackageVersion<blast_presale_package::BLAST_PRESALE_PACKAGE>,
    clock: &Clock,
)

/// Cancels only the admission-delay proposal.
public fun cancel_venue_delay(
    self: &mut CreationPolicy,
    package_version: &blast_admin::package_version::PackageVersion<blast_presale_package::BLAST_PRESALE_PACKAGE>,
    _: &blast_admin::blast_acl::AdminWitness<blast_core::blast_domain::CancelAdmin>,
)

/// Clears the requested pause bits once every presale delay and the core treasury's delay are
/// guarded: nonzero, with no pending change that would set one to zero and no pending admission.
/// A delay left at its zero publication value therefore blocks release instead of going live; the
/// generic administration then checks the upgrade delay and the pinned witness. Settlement,
/// cancellation, refunds and claims never require unpausing.
public fun unpause(
    self: &CreationPolicy,
    package_version: &mut blast_admin::package_version::PackageVersion<blast_presale_package::BLAST_PRESALE_PACKAGE>,
    unpause_admin: &blast_admin::blast_acl::AdminWitness<blast_admin::package_admin::UnpauseAdmin<blast_presale_package::BLAST_PRESALE_PACKAGE>>,
    package_admin: &blast_admin::package_admin::PackageAdmin<blast_presale_package::BLAST_PRESALE_PACKAGE>,
    fee_policy: &fee_policy::FeePolicy,
    treasury: &blast_core::blast_treasury::Treasury,
    bits: u8,
)

/// The presale's admission of the venue `Venue`, with which that venue borrows a sale token's
/// sealed `TreasuryCap` to prepare it (`blast_core::sealed_treasury_cap`). Aborts unless the
/// venue is admitted now.
public fun venue_admission<Venue: drop>(
    self: &CreationPolicy,
): blast_core::sealed_treasury_cap::VenueAdmission
```

### `blast_presale::events`

Presale event payloads and their package-only emitters. Pauses and versions emit from `blast_admin`, delayed settings from its delayed modules, partner acceptance from `blast_partners::partner_list`, and Blast's fee payments from `blast_core`. `PresaleCreated` stays beside the sale terms it records in `blast_presale`, which would otherwise form a module cycle.

Source: https://github.com/interest-protocol/blast-v2-contracts/blob/main/sui/launch/presale/sources/lib/events.move

#### Events

```move
/// Presales created afterward may (`allowed`) or may not raise `Quote`.
public struct QuoteAllowed<phantom Quote> has copy, drop {
    allowed: bool,
}

/// A presale was shared with these commitment and liquidity windows and its Blast partner, if
/// any. Commitments open at `start_ms`, which may be later than the share. A sale whose hard cap
/// closes it when filled may end earlier; `PresaleFunded` records when it did.
public struct PresaleOpened<phantom SaleToken, phantom Quote> has copy, drop {
    presale_id: ID,
    start_ms: u64,
    end_ms: u64,
    liquidity_deadline_ms: u64,
    uncapped_from_ms: u64,
    hard_cap_from_ms: Option<u64>,
    partner_id: Option<ID>,
}

/// Before the window started, Blast's `FeeAdmin` replaced this sale's Blast rates, moving Blast's
/// tokens to or from the sale allocation and its Quote to or from the LP's.
public struct ProtocolFeesChanged<phantom SaleToken, phantom Quote> has copy, drop {
    presale_id: ID,
    protocol_raise_bps: u64,
    protocol_token_bps: u64,
    protocol_lp_fee_meme_bps: u64,
    protocol_lp_fee_quote_bps: u64,
    /// Blast's shares of the raise and of the supply, paid on migration.
    protocol_quote: u64,
    protocol_tokens: u64,
    /// The tokens buyers now share pro rata.
    token_allocation: u64,
}

/// Quote joined a presale from the transaction sender.
public struct Committed<phantom SaleToken, phantom Quote> has copy, drop {
    presale_id: ID,
    quote: u64,
    wallet_commitment: u64,
    total_commitments: u64,
    participants: u64,
}

/// A funded presale closed at `end_ms` with these fixed claim totals; anyone may cancel it once
/// it is still unmigrated at `liquidity_deadline_ms`. Both are earlier than `PresaleOpened`'s
/// when the sale closed early.
public struct PresaleFunded<phantom SaleToken, phantom Quote> has copy, drop {
    presale_id: ID,
    total_commitments: u64,
    accepted_quote: u64,
    refundable_quote: u64,
    token_allocation: u64,
    end_ms: u64,
    liquidity_deadline_ms: u64,
}

/// A presale became fully refundable without releasing Quote.
public struct PresaleCancelled<phantom SaleToken, phantom Quote> has copy, drop {
    presale_id: ID,
    total_commitments: u64,
    refundable_quote: u64,
}

/// The raise left after Blast's and the recipients' shares, and the LP-token allocation, left
/// the sale in its hand-off to `venue`.
public struct PresaleMigrated<phantom SaleToken, phantom Quote> has copy, drop {
    presale_id: ID,
    venue: TypeName,
    liquidity_quote: u64,
    liquidity_tokens: u64,
    recipient_tokens: u64,
}

/// One configured recipient received its share of the raise, or the vesting schedule
/// `vesting_id` that holds it.
public struct RaiseAllocated<phantom SaleToken, phantom Quote> has copy, drop {
    presale_id: ID,
    recipient: address,
    quote: u64,
    vesting_id: Option<ID>,
}

/// One configured recipient received its immutable token allocation, or the vesting schedule
/// `vesting_id` that holds it.
public struct RecipientAllocated<phantom SaleToken, phantom Quote> has copy, drop {
    presale_id: ID,
    recipient: address,
    tokens: u64,
    vesting_id: Option<ID>,
}

/// `participant` received its token allocation, or the vesting schedule `vesting_id` that holds
/// it, and its Quote refund, leaving `remaining_participants` wallets still to claim.
public struct Claimed<phantom SaleToken, phantom Quote> has copy, drop {
    presale_id: ID,
    participant: address,
    wallet_commitment: u64,
    tokens: u64,
    quote_refund: u64,
    remaining_participants: u64,
    vesting_id: Option<ID>,
}
```

### `blast_presale::fee_policy`

Blast's fees on presales: a share of every successful raise, of every token supply, and of every collected LP fee.  Each presale snapshots the rates when it is created. Until the sale's window starts, the `FeeAdmin` may change that sale's rates, so its economics are fixed before commitments open; the LP-fee rates then pass to the sale's vault in its migration terms, which changes them per vault after delay. Blast's fees are paid to the one `blast_core::blast_treasury::Treasury`.

Source: https://github.com/interest-protocol/blast-v2-contracts/blob/main/sui/launch/presale/sources/config/fee_policy.move

#### Types

```move
/// Shared singleton holding the rates new presales snapshot.
public struct FeePolicy has key {
    id: UID,
    fees: blast_admin::delayed::Delayed<FeesKey, ProtocolFees>,
}

/// Keys the delayed lifecycle of the rates new presales snapshot.
public struct FeesKey() has copy, drop, store;

/// Blast's rates, in BPS: of the accepted raise, of the token supply, and of each collected
/// LP-fee asset.
public struct ProtocolFees has copy, drop, store {
    raise_bps: blast_core::bps::Bps,
    token_bps: blast_core::bps::Bps,
    lp_fee_meme_bps: blast_core::bps::Bps,
    lp_fee_quote_bps: blast_core::bps::Bps,
}
```

#### Functions

```move
/// Sets the rates presales created afterward snapshot; existing presales keep theirs.
/// Schedules fees after the full administrative delay.
public fun schedule_fees(
    self: &mut FeePolicy,
    package_version: &blast_admin::package_version::PackageVersion<blast_presale_package::BLAST_PRESALE_PACKAGE>,
    _: &blast_admin::blast_acl::AdminWitness<blast_core::blast_domain::FeeAdmin>,
    raise_bps: blast_core::bps::Bps,
    token_bps: blast_core::bps::Bps,
    lp_fee_meme_bps: blast_core::bps::Bps,
    lp_fee_quote_bps: blast_core::bps::Bps,
    delay_ms: u64,
    clock: &Clock,
)

/// Applies the scheduled fees permissionlessly once ready.
public fun apply_fees(
    self: &mut FeePolicy,
    package_version: &blast_admin::package_version::PackageVersion<blast_presale_package::BLAST_PRESALE_PACKAGE>,
    clock: &Clock,
)

/// Cancels a pending change, including after its delay has elapsed.
public fun cancel_fees(
    self: &mut FeePolicy,
    package_version: &blast_admin::package_version::PackageVersion<blast_presale_package::BLAST_PRESALE_PACKAGE>,
    _: &blast_admin::blast_acl::AdminWitness<blast_core::blast_domain::CancelAdmin>,
)
```

### `blast_presale::sale_token`

One-time-witness creation of a sale token whose `TreasuryCap` is sealed under the presale's witness from birth, so no one mints before the sale's `finalize` mints the whole supply.  A generated sale-token package's initializer calls `new` with its witness and delivers the sealed cap to the publisher, beside whatever it does with the currency's `MetadataCap`. The publisher hands the sealed cap to an admitted venue's `prepare`, which borrows it to reserve the pair's pool creation, and then to `blast_presale::new`, the only call that opens it.

Source: https://github.com/interest-protocol/blast-v2-contracts/blob/main/sui/launch/presale/sources/sale_token.move

#### Functions

```move
/// Registers the currency with the generated package's one-time witness and seals its
/// `TreasuryCap` under the presale's witness, returning the registry initializer for the
/// package to finalize beside it. Only that package's initializer holds the witness.
public fun new<SaleToken: drop>(
    otw: SaleToken,
    decimals: u8,
    symbol: String,
    name: String,
    description: String,
    icon_url: String,
    ctx: &mut TxContext,
): (coin_registry::CurrencyInitializer<SaleToken>, blast_core::sealed_treasury_cap::SealedTreasuryCap<SaleToken>)
```

## API reference: `blast_migrators` (Cetus and Bluefin venues)

Live on mainnet. This reference matches the published source.

Events: payload structs are emitted directly, each under its own type.

Abort codes: this package aborts with the numeric codes below from whichever module detects the failure; the abort location names that module.

| Code | Constant | Meaning |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 0 | `EInvalidCoinOrder` | `CoinA` and `CoinB` must follow the Cetus coin order. |
| 1 | `EPositionPoolMismatch` | The pool must be the vault's own pool. |
| 2 | `EWrongCoinPair` | `CoinA` and `CoinB` must be exactly the sale's Meme and Quote. |
| 3 | `EInvalidTier` | A Bluefin tier needs a tick spacing in 1..=400 and a fee rate in 1..=20,000. |
| 4 | `ESymbolTooLong` | A Bluefin Meme symbol must be at most 32 bytes. |
| 5 | `EInvalidAutoLpPrice` | Auto-LP needs positive ordered bounds containing the current pool sqrt price. |

### `blast_migrators::blast_migrators`

The migrators package's publication.  `init` turns the one-time witness into `blast_admin`'s owned `Publication`, which the publisher wraps with the package's `UpgradeCap` through `blast_admin::package_admin::wrap`; that shares `PackageVersion<BLAST_MIGRATORS>` and `PackageAdmin<BLAST_MIGRATORS>`. Venues define no pause bits, so the version starts unpaused and the package has no unpause. Residual risk: the package has no emergency stop of its own. Migration stops with each product's migration pause bit and `buyback_and_burn` with the vault's admin bit, but `prepare`, `collect_fees`, and `settle_and_apply_fee_split` can be stopped only by advancing this package's version through a delayed upgrade, which retires every call to the old bytecode. Upgrades are scheduled, authorized and committed through the generic `package_admin` under this package's own `UpgradeAdmin<BLAST_MIGRATORS>` role. The products never import this package: each venue redeems the `Migration` its product's own `migrate` returned earlier in the transaction.

Source: https://github.com/interest-protocol/blast-v2-contracts/blob/main/sui/launch/migrators/sources/blast_migrators.move

#### Types

```move
/// The package's one-time witness (OTW), typing its version, administration, and roles.
public struct BLAST_MIGRATORS() has drop;

/// The package witness `blast_admin` pins at publication, the one type that authorizes this
/// package's own administration calls such as unpause. Only this module could construct it, and it
/// is never stored or returned; the package defines no pause bits, so nothing constructs it.
public struct PackageWitness() has drop;
```

### `blast_migrators::bluefin`

Bluefin coin preparation, migration into `blast_vault` custody, fee collection, separate auto-LP compounding, settled fee-split updates, and buybacks over custodied concentrated-liquidity principal, for launches and presales alike.  Bluefin pools are keyed by their exact type-parameter order, so this module always creates and expects `Pool<Meme, Quote>` with Meme as coin A.

Source: https://github.com/interest-protocol/blast-v2-contracts/blob/main/sui/launch/migrators/sources/venues/bluefin.move

#### Types

```move
/// The Bluefin venue's sealed witness: it issues tickets, redeems migrations, and keys the position
/// in the vault, so each product's venue list and the vault registry admit this one type.
public struct Venue() has drop;

/// A prepared coin's Bluefin pool tier.
public struct PoolTier has copy, drop, store {
    tick_spacing: u32,
    fee_rate: u64,
}

/// Sealed `VenueConfig` field name for the Meme type's Bluefin pool tier.
public struct PoolTierKey<phantom Meme>() has copy, drop, store;
```

#### Functions

```move
/// Prepares `Meme` for a Bluefin sale in `Quote` at the creator's tier, recording the tier, and
/// returns the ticket the product's `new` redeems. The coin's sealed `TreasuryCap` proves the
/// caller controls the coin, so no one else can record its tier first; Bluefin reserves nothing,
/// so the cap is never borrowed.
///
/// Bluefin checks the tier only when migration creates the pool, and a sale cannot change its
/// tier, so the same bounds apply here first. The pool and its position store the Meme symbol, so
/// it is bounded here too.
public fun prepare<Meme, Quote>(
    migrators_version: &blast_admin::package_version::PackageVersion<blast_migrators::BLAST_MIGRATORS>,
    venue_config: &mut venue_config::VenueConfig,
    _: &blast_core::sealed_treasury_cap::SealedTreasuryCap<Meme>,
    currency: &Currency<Meme>,
    tick_spacing: u32,
    fee_rate: u64,
    _: &mut TxContext,
): blast_core::migration::VenueTicket<Meme, Quote>

/// Migrates a sale into a new full-range pool at the recorded tier and a `blast_vault` vault:
/// `migration` is the hand-off the launch's or presale's own `migrate` returned earlier in the
/// transaction.
///
/// Redeems the hand-off. The opening price derives from the handed-off balances, which
/// seed the position on the side `is_meme_seed_side` chooses. `creation_fee` is paid whole, and
/// Bluefin aborts unless it is exactly the configured SUI fee. The position and the venue
/// residuals go to the vault, which has no withdrawal path. The caller receives no object; Bluefin
/// records it as the new pool's manager, a role limited to the pool's reward emissions and to
/// handing the role on (ADR 0088).
public fun migrate<Meme, Quote>(
    migration: blast_core::migration::Migration<Meme, Quote>,
    migrators_version: &blast_admin::package_version::PackageVersion<blast_migrators::BLAST_MIGRATORS>,
    venue_config: &venue_config::VenueConfig,
    currency: &Currency<Meme>,
    quote_currency: &Currency<Quote>,
    bluefin_config: &mut config::GlobalConfig,
    registry: &mut blast_vault::blast_vault_package::VaultRegistry,
    vault_version: &blast_admin::package_version::PackageVersion<blast_vault::blast_vault_package::BLAST_VAULT_PACKAGE>,
    treasury: &blast_core::blast_treasury::Treasury,
    creation_fee: Coin<sui::SUI>,
    clock: &Clock,
    ctx: &mut TxContext,
)

/// Collects the custodied position's fees, splits them, and burns the burn share's Meme.
/// Anyone may collect; the auto-LP share waits in the vault for the separate `AutoLpAdmin` entry.
public fun collect_fees<Meme, Quote>(
    self: &mut blast_vault::vault::Vault<Meme, Quote>,
    migrators_version: &blast_admin::package_version::PackageVersion<blast_migrators::BLAST_MIGRATORS>,
    vault_version: &blast_admin::package_version::PackageVersion<blast_vault::blast_vault_package::BLAST_VAULT_PACKAGE>,
    treasury: &blast_core::blast_treasury::Treasury,
    currency: &mut Currency<Meme>,
    bluefin_config: &config::GlobalConfig,
    pool: &mut pool::Pool<Meme, Quote>,
    clock: &Clock,
    ctx: &mut TxContext,
)

/// Anyone may settle accrued fees under the current split, then apply the stored ready proposal.
public fun settle_and_apply_fee_split<Meme, Quote>(
    self: &mut blast_vault::vault::Vault<Meme, Quote>,
    migrators_version: &blast_admin::package_version::PackageVersion<blast_migrators::BLAST_MIGRATORS>,
    vault_version: &blast_admin::package_version::PackageVersion<blast_vault::blast_vault_package::BLAST_VAULT_PACKAGE>,
    treasury: &blast_core::blast_treasury::Treasury,
    currency: &mut Currency<Meme>,
    bluefin_config: &config::GlobalConfig,
    pool: &mut pool::Pool<Meme, Quote>,
    clock: &Clock,
    ctx: &mut TxContext,
)

/// Swaps `quote_in` of the vault's burn Quote for Meme in its pool and burns it, under a buyback
/// admin. Aborts unless `quote_in` is positive and at most the burn Quote held, and unless the swap
/// returns at least `min_meme_out`; the admin sets it from an off-chain price, since anyone can move
/// the pool's price within a transaction. Quote the pool does not take stays for the next buyback.
public fun buyback_and_burn<Meme, Quote>(
    self: &mut blast_vault::vault::Vault<Meme, Quote>,
    migrators_version: &blast_admin::package_version::PackageVersion<blast_migrators::BLAST_MIGRATORS>,
    vault_version: &blast_admin::package_version::PackageVersion<blast_vault::blast_vault_package::BLAST_VAULT_PACKAGE>,
    admin_witness: &blast_admin::blast_acl::AdminWitness<blast_core::blast_domain::BuybackAdmin>,
    currency: &mut Currency<Meme>,
    bluefin_config: &config::GlobalConfig,
    pool: &mut pool::Pool<Meme, Quote>,
    quote_in: u64,
    min_meme_out: u64,
    clock: &Clock,
    ctx: &mut TxContext,
)

/// Reinvests waiting auto-LP fees under a live `AutoLpAdmin` capability. The admin derives
/// inclusive Q64.64 sqrt-price bounds from a trusted off-chain quote, in the pool's coin order.
/// Invalid bounds or a price outside them abort before taking any vault balances. Collection
/// stays permissionless and independent, so a refused add never blocks fee collection.
///
/// Taking the pool by value and re-sharing it prevents later PTB reuse.
/// Private entry restricts Move callers and runtime-sensitive PTB compositions; it does not
/// isolate swaps earlier in the transaction. Authorization and price bounds are required together.
entry fun compound_auto_lp<Meme, Quote>(
    self: &mut blast_vault::vault::Vault<Meme, Quote>,
    migrators_version: &blast_admin::package_version::PackageVersion<blast_migrators::BLAST_MIGRATORS>,
    vault_version: &blast_admin::package_version::PackageVersion<blast_vault::blast_vault_package::BLAST_VAULT_PACKAGE>,
    acl: &blast_admin::blast_acl::ACL,
    admin_cap: &blast_admin::blast_acl::AdminCap,
    bluefin_config: &config::GlobalConfig,
    pool: pool::Pool<Meme, Quote>,
    min_sqrt_price: u128,
    max_sqrt_price: u128,
    clock: &Clock,
    _: &mut TxContext,
)
```

### `blast_migrators::cetus`

Cetus coin preparation, pool-creation reservation, migration into `blast_vault` custody, and collection, for launches and presales alike.  Anyone holding Meme can create a Cetus pool, and Cetus allows one pool per pair and tick spacing, so a buyer could pre-create the migration pool at a price of their choosing. Preparing the coin therefore mints the Meme type's single Cetus `PoolCreationCap` from its treasury capability, borrowed from the product's seal with that product's admission of this venue, before any Meme can leave the sale's custody, registers the pair at the creator's tick spacing, and returns a `VenueTicket` for that pair the sale redeems; afterwards only that capability or Cetus's pool-manager role can create the pair's pool there. Trusting that role is a venue-operator assumption: a pool it creates first makes the pair's migration abort, and only a migrators upgrade could recover the sale. The capability stays in `VenueConfig` under a key only this module constructs, with no extraction or unregistration path.  Migration redeems the `Migration` the sale's own `migrate` returned earlier in the transaction and burns the full-range position through Cetus LP Burn, so Cetus itself records the sale's liquidity as permanently locked; the `blast_vault` vault keeps the `CetusLPBurnProof`, which can only collect fees. A burned position cannot receive liquidity, so auto-LP adds to a second full-range position the vault opens on first use and keeps with no removal path.  Cetus orders a pool's coin types by type name. Each route after `prepare` takes the pool's coin types as `CoinA` and `CoinB` beside the sale's `Meme` and `Quote`, proves they are the same pair, and retypes balances between the two orders through a checked dynamic-field round trip (ADR 0100).

Source: https://github.com/interest-protocol/blast-v2-contracts/blob/main/sui/launch/migrators/sources/venues/cetus.move

#### Types

