```move
/// Cancels the pending admission-delay change, even a ready one.
public fun cancel_witness_delay(
    self: &mut VaultRegistry,
    _: &blast_admin::blast_acl::AdminWitness<blast_core::blast_domain::CancelAdmin>,
    package_version: &blast_admin::package_version::PackageVersion<BLAST_VAULT_PACKAGE>,
)

/// Clears pause bits once the witness admission delay and the core treasury's delay are guarded:
/// nonzero, with no pending change that would set one to zero and no pending admission. It runs
/// only on the live version; the generic administration then checks the upgrade delay and the
/// pinned witness.
public fun unpause(
    self: &VaultRegistry,
    unpause_admin: &blast_admin::blast_acl::AdminWitness<blast_admin::package_admin::UnpauseAdmin<BLAST_VAULT_PACKAGE>>,
    package_admin: &blast_admin::package_admin::PackageAdmin<BLAST_VAULT_PACKAGE>,
    package_version: &mut blast_admin::package_version::PackageVersion<BLAST_VAULT_PACKAGE>,
    treasury: &blast_core::blast_treasury::Treasury,
    bits: u8,
)

/// Ends the vault package's upgrades once its scheduled immutability action is ready (#195,
/// after the audit). It runs only on the live version; the generic administration then checks
/// the package witness, consumes the action and destroys the UpgradeCap.
public fun make_immutable(
    package_admin: &mut blast_admin::package_admin::PackageAdmin<BLAST_VAULT_PACKAGE>,
    upgrade_admin: &blast_admin::blast_acl::AdminWitness<blast_admin::package_admin::UpgradeAdmin<BLAST_VAULT_PACKAGE>>,
    package_version: &blast_admin::package_version::PackageVersion<BLAST_VAULT_PACKAGE>,
    clock: &Clock,
)
```

### `blast_vault::constants`

Package-wide vault constants exposed as zero-cost macros.

Source: https://github.com/interest-protocol/blast-v2-contracts/blob/main/sui/launch/vault/sources/lib/constants.move

#### Constants

- `version: u64 = 1`: The package version this bytecode serves.
- `admin_pause_bit: u8 = 1`: Stops Blast's administrative operations: protocol-share changes, holder-reward rounds, gas settlement, and buyback. Venue admission, collection, owner actions, claims, closes, and cancels never pause.
- `valid_pause_bits: u8 = blast_vault::constants::admin_pause_bit!()`: Every supported vault pause flag.
- `witness_admission_delay_ms: u64 = 0`: Initial witness admission delay: zero, so Blast lists its products and venues at launch while publication keeps the package paused. `blast_vault_package::unpause` refuses until a nonzero delay applies.
- `fee_change_delay_ms: u64 = 48 * 60 * 60 * 1_000`: Fixed delay before a vault's fee split or Blast share changes; no call can change it.
- `round_delay_ms: u64 = 2 * 60 * 60 * 1_000`: Initial delay before a scheduled holder-reward round may publish, or its next delay period may change: 2 hours, so a cancel administrator can stop a bad round while daily rounds fit.
- `max_gas_reserve_bps: u64 = 1_000`: Largest holder-reward gas reserve, in basis points of the round's holder Quote.
- `max_reward_leaves: u64 = 65_536`: The most leaves one holder-reward round may commit.
- `digest_length: u64 = 32`: Byte length of a round's Merkle root and manifest digest.
- `reward_leaf_domain: vector<u8> = b"blast-holder-rewards-v2"`: The domain every holder-reward leaf hashes under.

### `blast_vault::holder_rewards`

Holder rewards: rounds funded from a vault's holder-reward route and paid by Merkle proof.  Blast's off-chain system turns checkpointed holder balances into a Merkle allocation, and a `RewardsAdmin` schedules it as a round on the vault. Once the vault's round delay has elapsed, anyone publishes it, reserving its Meme and Quote budget from the vault. Anyone can relay a proof afterwards; each leaf pays its committed beneficiary once, and anyone closes the round at its deadline, returning what is left to the vault.  A claim pays by transfer, so a leaf's beneficiary must be an account: a coin sent to an object's address (a pool, a vesting schedule, another vault) has no recovery path, and the claimed leaf never returns to the vault. Nothing on chain tells the two apart, so the allocation leaves object-held balances out.

Source: https://github.com/interest-protocol/blast-v2-contracts/blob/main/sui/launch/vault/sources/holder_rewards.move

#### Types

```move
/// A published round: independently funded claims with optional, one-shot gas custody that claims
/// never touch, returned to its vault at the claim deadline.
public struct Round<phantom Meme, phantom Quote> has key {
    id: UID,
    vault_id: ID,
    number: u64,
    claim_deadline_at_ms: u64,
    root: vector<u8>,
    leaf_count: u64,
    claimed_bitmap: vector<u256>,
    meme: Balance<Meme>,
    quote: Balance<Quote>,
    treasury: address,
    gas_reserve: Option<Balance<Quote>>,
}
```

#### Functions

```move
/// Schedules a round's terms for publication after the vault's applied round delay, replacing
/// any scheduled round or delay change. The terms' static checks run now; the round number,
/// interval, and budget are checked against the vault now to fail fast and again at publication.
/// A zero reserve disables gas reimbursement. Gas attribution remains off chain.
public fun schedule_round<Meme, Quote>(
    vault: &mut vault::Vault<Meme, Quote>,
    _: &blast_admin::blast_acl::AdminWitness<blast_core::blast_domain::RewardsAdmin>,
    package_version: &blast_admin::package_version::PackageVersion<blast_vault_package::BLAST_VAULT_PACKAGE>,
    number: u64,
    start_checkpoint: u64,
    end_checkpoint: u64,
    root: vector<u8>,
    manifest_digest: vector<u8>,
    leaf_count: u64,
    meme_amount: u64,
    quote_amount: u64,
    gas_reserve_amount: u64,
    claim_window_duration_ms: u64,
    expected_treasury: address,
    clock: &Clock,
)

/// Publishes the scheduled round once its delay has elapsed: the treasury must still be the
/// expected one, and the round's number, interval, and budget must still fit the vault. Funds
/// holder claims and optional gas custody separately, with claims open for the scheduled window
/// from now, and shares the round. Anyone may call it.
public fun publish_round<Meme, Quote>(
    vault: &mut vault::Vault<Meme, Quote>,
    package_version: &blast_admin::package_version::PackageVersion<blast_vault_package::BLAST_VAULT_PACKAGE>,
    treasury: &blast_core::blast_treasury::Treasury,
    clock: &Clock,
    ctx: &mut TxContext,
)

/// Cancels the scheduled round, even a ready one; nothing was reserved, so no balance moves.
public fun cancel_round<Meme, Quote>(
    vault: &mut vault::Vault<Meme, Quote>,
    _: &blast_admin::blast_acl::AdminWitness<blast_core::blast_domain::CancelAdmin>,
    package_version: &blast_admin::package_version::PackageVersion<blast_vault_package::BLAST_VAULT_PACKAGE>,
)

/// Schedules a new round delay after the applied one, replacing any scheduled round.
public fun schedule_round_delay<Meme, Quote>(
    vault: &mut vault::Vault<Meme, Quote>,
    _: &blast_admin::blast_acl::AdminWitness<blast_core::blast_domain::RewardsAdmin>,
    package_version: &blast_admin::package_version::PackageVersion<blast_vault_package::BLAST_VAULT_PACKAGE>,
    delay_ms: u64,
    clock: &Clock,
)

/// Applies a ready round-delay change; anyone may call it.
public fun apply_round_delay<Meme, Quote>(
    vault: &mut vault::Vault<Meme, Quote>,
    package_version: &blast_admin::package_version::PackageVersion<blast_vault_package::BLAST_VAULT_PACKAGE>,
    clock: &Clock,
)

/// Cancels the pending round-delay change, even a ready one.
public fun cancel_round_delay<Meme, Quote>(
    vault: &mut vault::Vault<Meme, Quote>,
    _: &blast_admin::blast_acl::AdminWitness<blast_core::blast_domain::CancelAdmin>,
    package_version: &blast_admin::package_version::PackageVersion<blast_vault_package::BLAST_VAULT_PACKAGE>,
)

/// Pays one committed allocation before the claim deadline, only to its committed beneficiary;
/// anyone may relay an unchanged proof. Claims never pause.
public fun claim<Meme, Quote>(
    self: &mut Round<Meme, Quote>,
    package_version: &blast_admin::package_version::PackageVersion<blast_vault_package::BLAST_VAULT_PACKAGE>,
    index: u64,
    beneficiary: address,
    meme_amount: u64,
    quote_amount: u64,
    proof: vector<vector<u8>>,
    clock: &Clock,
    ctx: &mut TxContext,
)

/// Settles a measured, capped gas reimbursement once: the round's treasury receives it, and all
/// unused reserve returns to the vault at once.
public fun settle_gas_reserve<Meme, Quote>(
    self: &mut Round<Meme, Quote>,
    _: &blast_admin::blast_acl::AdminWitness<blast_core::blast_domain::RewardsAdmin>,
    package_version: &blast_admin::package_version::PackageVersion<blast_vault_package::BLAST_VAULT_PACKAGE>,
    vault: &mut vault::Vault<Meme, Quote>,
    reimbursement_amount: u64,
    ctx: &mut TxContext,
)

/// Closes the round at or after its claim deadline, returning every unclaimed holder balance and
/// any unsettled reserve to its vault, so missing settlement authority cannot strand custody.
/// Anyone may call it.
public fun close_round<Meme, Quote>(
    self: Round<Meme, Quote>,
    package_version: &blast_admin::package_version::PackageVersion<blast_vault_package::BLAST_VAULT_PACKAGE>,
    vault: &mut vault::Vault<Meme, Quote>,
    clock: &Clock,
    _: &mut TxContext,
)
```

### `blast_vault::vault`

The LP vault every Blast sale migrates into: custody of a venue position with no exposed withdrawal path, and the split of every fee collection it earns.  While the vault or its venue package remains upgradeable, authorized upgrades can change custody behavior. Cetus separately burns the migration position through its LP Burn contract; the vault holds that burn proof and any unburned auto-LP position.  An admitted venue builds the vault from the terms it redeemed from a sale's migration, attaches the position under its sealed `Venue` key, and shares it. Every collection then pays Blast its per-vault share through the core protocol ledger, at once or held for the sale's partner, and divides the rest by the vault's fee split: address routes are paid at once, and the owner, auto-LP, holder-reward, and burn routes accrue here. The owner acts with an `OwnerProof` from the sale's product; Blast acts through its roles; venue-only operations require the venue's witness, which only the venue module constructs.

Source: https://github.com/interest-protocol/blast-v2-contracts/blob/main/sui/launch/vault/sources/vault.move

#### Types

```move
/// Derives a sale's one vault from the registry: the witness that sealed its terms and the sale.
public struct VaultKey has copy, drop, store {
    package_witness: TypeName,
    origin: ID,
}

/// The key under which the venue's migration position is held; only `Venue` can reach it.
public struct PositionKey<phantom Venue>() has copy, drop, store;

/// The key for the separate position auto-LP adds to on venues whose migration position cannot
/// receive more liquidity (Cetus burns it).
public struct AutoLpPositionKey<phantom Venue>() has copy, drop, store;

/// Keys the fee split's delayed lifecycle.
public struct FeeSplitKey() has copy, drop, store;

/// Keys Blast's per-vault share's delayed lifecycle.
public struct ProtocolBpsKey() has copy, drop, store;

/// Keys the holder-reward round publication's delayed lifecycle; its action is the round's terms.
public struct RoundPublicationKey() has copy, drop, store;

/// The witness that creates every vault's protocol ledger; only this module constructs it.
public struct VaultWitness() has drop;

/// What a venue took from one vault's burn Quote for a buyback; `burn_buyback` on the same vault
/// consumes it, so the Quote spent is measured here, not reported by the venue.
public struct BuybackReceipt {
    vault_id: ID,
    taken_quote: u64,
}

/// What a venue took from one vault's auto-LP balances; `keep_auto_lp` on the same vault consumes
/// it, so the liquidity added is measured here, not reported by the venue.
public struct AutoLpReceipt {
    vault_id: ID,
    taken_meme: u64,
    taken_quote: u64,
}

/// Blast's share of each LP-fee collection, per asset, each at most 3,000 basis points.
public struct ProtocolBps has copy, drop, store {
    meme_bps: blast_core::bps::Bps,
    quote_bps: blast_core::bps::Bps,
}

/// The committed terms of one holder-reward round, checked when built: two 32-byte commitments, 1
/// to 65,536 leaves, a nonempty checkpoint interval and budget, a holder Quote and gas reserve that
/// sum within u64 with the reserve at most 10% of the holder Quote, a nonzero expected treasury,
/// and a positive claim window. The claim window is a duration; publication starts it.
public struct RoundTerms has copy, drop, store {
    number: u64,
    start_checkpoint: u64,
    end_checkpoint: u64,
    root: vector<u8>,
    manifest_digest: vector<u8>,
    leaf_count: u64,
    meme_amount: u64,
    quote_amount: u64,
    gas_reserve_amount: u64,
    claim_window_duration_ms: u64,
    expected_treasury: address,
}

/// Shared custody for one migrated sale's position and the balances its LP fees are split into.
/// A holder-reward round waits in `round_publication` for its applied delay before anyone may
/// publish it; at most one round is scheduled at a time.
public struct Vault<phantom Meme, phantom Quote> has key {
    id: UID,
    /// The terms the vault was created with; owner proofs are checked against them.
    terms: blast_core::migration::VaultTerms,
    /// The admitted venue fixed at construction; only its witness may attach the position.
    venue: TypeName,
    pool_id: ID,
    fee_split: blast_admin::delayed::Delayed<FeeSplitKey, blast_core::fee_split::FeeSplit>,
    protocol_bps: blast_admin::delayed::Delayed<ProtocolBpsKey, ProtocolBps>,
    protocol: blast_core::protocol_fees::Ledger<Meme, Quote>,
    owner_meme: Balance<Meme>,
    owner_quote: Balance<Quote>,
    auto_lp_meme: Balance<Meme>,
    auto_lp_quote: Balance<Quote>,
    rewards_meme: Balance<Meme>,
    rewards_quote: Balance<Quote>,
    burn_quote: Balance<Quote>,
    next_round: u64,
    last_checkpoint: Option<u64>,
    round_publication: blast_admin::delayed_action::DelayedAction<RoundPublicationKey, RoundTerms>,
}
```

#### Functions

```move
/// Builds a vault for `terms`, unshared so the venue attaches its position and settles the
/// migration residuals in `share_with_position` before anyone can reach it. Aborts unless `Venue`
/// and the terms' sealing witness are admitted. The vault is permanently bound to `Venue`.
/// Blast's share needs no check, since `migration::vault_terms` capped it when the terms were
/// sealed. The vault's ID derives from the
/// registry, the sealing witness, and the sale, so each sale has exactly one vault and a client
/// computes its address from the sale alone. The vault has `key` without `store`, so it cannot
/// leave this transaction except through `share_with_position`.
public fun new<Meme, Quote, Venue>(
    registry: &mut blast_vault_package::VaultRegistry,
    _: &Venue,
    package_version: &blast_admin::package_version::PackageVersion<blast_vault_package::BLAST_VAULT_PACKAGE>,
    terms: blast_core::migration::VaultTerms,
    pool_id: ID,
    _: &mut TxContext,
): Vault<Meme, Quote>

/// Attaches `position`, holding `liquidity`, under the venue's sealed key with no removal path,
/// settles the migration residuals by the terms' policy, and shares the vault. Protocol residuals
/// are paid to the treasury at once, or held for the partner; owner residuals wait for the owner.
/// Aborts unless `Venue` is the venue bound by `new`. Its position key can be attached only once,
/// so an already shared vault cannot be initialized again.
public fun share_with_position<Meme, Quote, Venue, Position: key + store>(
    self: Vault<Meme, Quote>,
    _: &Venue,
    treasury: &blast_core::blast_treasury::Treasury,
    position: Position,
    liquidity: u128,
    residual_meme: Balance<Meme>,
    residual_quote: Balance<Quote>,
    ctx: &mut TxContext,
)

/// Borrows the venue's migration position mutably, with no removal path. Another venue's key finds
/// no field, so the borrow aborts.
public fun position_mut<Meme, Quote, Venue, Position: key + store>(
    self: &mut Vault<Meme, Quote>,
    _: &Venue,
    package_version: &blast_admin::package_version::PackageVersion<blast_vault_package::BLAST_VAULT_PACKAGE>,
): &mut Position

/// Whether the venue's auto-LP position exists.
public fun has_auto_lp_position<Meme, Quote, Venue>(self: &Vault<Meme, Quote>, _: &Venue): bool

/// Attaches the venue's auto-LP position once, with no removal path.
public fun attach_auto_lp_position<Meme, Quote, Venue, Position: key + store>(
    self: &mut Vault<Meme, Quote>,
    _: &Venue,
    package_version: &blast_admin::package_version::PackageVersion<blast_vault_package::BLAST_VAULT_PACKAGE>,
    position: Position,
)

/// Borrows the venue's auto-LP position.
public fun auto_lp_position<Meme, Quote, Venue, Position: key + store>(
    self: &Vault<Meme, Quote>,
    _: &Venue,
): &Position

/// Borrows the venue's auto-LP position mutably, with no removal path.
public fun auto_lp_position_mut<Meme, Quote, Venue, Position: key + store>(
    self: &mut Vault<Meme, Quote>,
    _: &Venue,
    package_version: &blast_admin::package_version::PackageVersion<blast_vault_package::BLAST_VAULT_PACKAGE>,
): &mut Position

/// Splits one collection of the position's fees: Blast's share of each asset, rounded up, into the
/// protocol ledger, paid to the treasury at once unless the sale named a partner; then the rest by
/// the fee split, in route order. Address routes are paid at once; the owner, auto-LP, and
/// holder-reward routes accrue; the burn route's Meme is burned and its Quote kept for buyback. A
/// zero collection moves and emits nothing.
public fun split_fees<Meme, Quote, Venue>(
    self: &mut Vault<Meme, Quote>,
    _: &Venue,
    package_version: &blast_admin::package_version::PackageVersion<blast_vault_package::BLAST_VAULT_PACKAGE>,
    treasury: &blast_core::blast_treasury::Treasury,
    currency: &mut Currency<Meme>,
    meme: Balance<Meme>,
    quote: Balance<Quote>,
    ctx: &mut TxContext,
)

/// Aborts unless the scheduled fee split is ready; a venue checks this before settling fees under
/// the current split.
public fun assert_fee_split_ready<Meme, Quote>(self: &Vault<Meme, Quote>, clock: &Clock)

/// Applies the ready fee split after the venue has settled the fees accrued under the current one,
/// so each fee follows the split it accrued under.
public fun apply_fee_split<Meme, Quote, Venue>(
    self: &mut Vault<Meme, Quote>,
    _: &Venue,
    package_version: &blast_admin::package_version::PackageVersion<blast_vault_package::BLAST_VAULT_PACKAGE>,
    clock: &Clock,
)

/// Takes every Meme and Quote waiting to be added back to the position, with the receipt
/// `keep_auto_lp` must consume, even when both are zero.
public fun take_auto_lp<Meme, Quote, Venue>(
    self: &mut Vault<Meme, Quote>,
    _: &Venue,
    package_version: &blast_admin::package_version::PackageVersion<blast_vault_package::BLAST_VAULT_PACKAGE>,
): (Balance<Meme>, Balance<Quote>, AutoLpReceipt)

/// Consumes the auto-LP receipt, keeps what the pool did not take for the next call, and records
/// what it added: the receipt's amounts less what returns. Aborts unless the receipt is this
/// vault's and each return is at most what was taken.
public fun keep_auto_lp<Meme, Quote, Venue>(
    self: &mut Vault<Meme, Quote>,
    _: &Venue,
    package_version: &blast_admin::package_version::PackageVersion<blast_vault_package::BLAST_VAULT_PACKAGE>,
    receipt: AutoLpReceipt,
    meme: Balance<Meme>,
    quote: Balance<Quote>,
)

/// Takes exactly `quote_in` of the Quote waiting to buy Meme back, under a buyback administrator,
/// with the receipt `burn_buyback` must consume. Aborts unless `quote_in` is positive and at most
/// the burn Quote held.
public fun take_buyback_quote<Meme, Quote, Venue>(
    self: &mut Vault<Meme, Quote>,
    _: &Venue,
    _: &blast_admin::blast_acl::AdminWitness<blast_core::blast_domain::BuybackAdmin>,
    package_version: &blast_admin::package_version::PackageVersion<blast_vault_package::BLAST_VAULT_PACKAGE>,
    quote_in: u64,
): (Balance<Quote>, BuybackReceipt)

/// Consumes the buyback receipt, burns the Meme the swap returned, which must reach
/// `min_meme_out`, and keeps the Quote the pool did not take for the next buyback. Aborts unless
/// the receipt is this vault's and the unspent Quote is at most what was taken.
public fun burn_buyback<Meme, Quote, Venue>(
    self: &mut Vault<Meme, Quote>,
    _: &Venue,
    package_version: &blast_admin::package_version::PackageVersion<blast_vault_package::BLAST_VAULT_PACKAGE>,
    receipt: BuybackReceipt,
    currency: &mut Currency<Meme>,
    meme: Balance<Meme>,
    unspent_quote: Balance<Quote>,
    min_meme_out: u64,
    ctx: &mut TxContext,
)

/// Claims the owner's accrued LP fees and owner residuals with an owner proof from the sale's
/// product. Aborts when nothing has accrued.
public fun claim_owner_balances<Meme, Quote>(
    self: &mut Vault<Meme, Quote>,
    proof: blast_core::owner_proof::OwnerProof,
    package_version: &blast_admin::package_version::PackageVersion<blast_vault_package::BLAST_VAULT_PACKAGE>,
    ctx: &mut TxContext,
): (Coin<Meme>, Coin<Quote>)

/// Proposes a new fee split with an owner proof, ready after the fixed 48-hour delay.
public fun schedule_fee_split<Meme, Quote>(
    self: &mut Vault<Meme, Quote>,
    proof: blast_core::owner_proof::OwnerProof,
    package_version: &blast_admin::package_version::PackageVersion<blast_vault_package::BLAST_VAULT_PACKAGE>,
    proposed: blast_core::fee_split::FeeSplit,
    clock: &Clock,
)

/// Cancels the pending fee split with an owner proof, even a ready one.
public fun cancel_fee_split<Meme, Quote>(
    self: &mut Vault<Meme, Quote>,
    proof: blast_core::owner_proof::OwnerProof,
    package_version: &blast_admin::package_version::PackageVersion<blast_vault_package::BLAST_VAULT_PACKAGE>,
)

/// Proposes Blast's per-vault share, each asset at most 3,000 basis points, ready after the fixed
/// 48-hour delay; it applies to fees collected after it is applied.
public fun schedule_protocol_bps<Meme, Quote>(
    self: &mut Vault<Meme, Quote>,
    _: &blast_admin::blast_acl::AdminWitness<blast_core::blast_domain::FeeAdmin>,
    package_version: &blast_admin::package_version::PackageVersion<blast_vault_package::BLAST_VAULT_PACKAGE>,
    meme_bps: blast_core::bps::Bps,
    quote_bps: blast_core::bps::Bps,
    clock: &Clock,
)

/// Applies Blast's ready per-vault share; anyone may call it.
public fun apply_protocol_bps<Meme, Quote>(
    self: &mut Vault<Meme, Quote>,
    package_version: &blast_admin::package_version::PackageVersion<blast_vault_package::BLAST_VAULT_PACKAGE>,
    clock: &Clock,
)

/// Cancels Blast's pending per-vault share, even a ready one.
public fun cancel_protocol_bps<Meme, Quote>(
    self: &mut Vault<Meme, Quote>,
    _: &blast_admin::blast_acl::AdminWitness<blast_core::blast_domain::CancelAdmin>,
    package_version: &blast_admin::package_version::PackageVersion<blast_vault_package::BLAST_VAULT_PACKAGE>,
)

/// Pays the Blast fees held for the sale's partner: the partner its cut of each asset, then the
/// treasury the rest. Anyone may call it, since both payees are fixed; holding nothing moves and
/// emits nothing.
public fun pay_protocol_fees_with_partner<Meme, Quote>(
    self: &mut Vault<Meme, Quote>,
    package_version: &blast_admin::package_version::PackageVersion<blast_vault_package::BLAST_VAULT_PACKAGE>,
    treasury: &blast_core::blast_treasury::Treasury,
    partner: &blast_partners::blast_partners::Partner,
    ctx: &mut TxContext,
)

/// The pool the vault's position belongs to; venues check collections come from it.
public fun pool_id<Meme, Quote>(self: &Vault<Meme, Quote>): ID
```

## API reference: `blast_partners` (Partner codes)

Live on mainnet. This reference matches the published source.

Events: payload structs are emitted directly, each under its own type.

Abort codes: this package aborts with the numeric codes below from whichever module detects the failure; the abort location names that module.

| Code | Constant | Meaning |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 0 | `EInvalidRecipient` | A partner's recipient must not be the zero address. |
| 1 | `EShareAboveMax` | A partner's share must be at most 80% of Blast's fee. |
| 2 | `EUnchanged` | An update must change the value it sets or proposes. |
| 3 | `ENotLiveCap` | Only the partner's applied cap may set its recipient. |
| 4 | `EPartnerNotAccepted` | The partner list does not accept the partner now. |

### `blast_partners::blast_partners`

Blast partner codes, shared by every Blast product.  A partner is one deal: it is paid a share of the fee Blast charges on every trade that names it, in the same transaction. A company holds one `PartnerCap` for all its deals, each a separate partner with its own share. Blast's partner administrator registers partners under a cap, proposes each one's share, and can propose moving a partner to another cap; the cap's holder sets where each of its partners is paid. Initial terms apply at registration. A later share or cap applies only when someone applies it, no sooner than `PARTNER_CHANGE_DELAY_MS` after it was proposed. That delay is fixed: this module never changes it, and the package is published immutable. The recipient the cap holder sets applies at once.  Products call `pay` with Blast's own fee on a trade. `pay` emits `PartnerPaid` for what the partner received; the product records the trade and the amount in its own event. Share and cap lifecycle events come from `blast_admin::delayed`, keyed by `ShareKey` and `CapKey` and owned by the partner.

Source: https://github.com/interest-protocol/blast-v2-contracts/blob/main/sui/launch/partners/sources/blast_partners.move

#### Types

```move
/// The one administrator role in the shared Blast ACL: register partners, and
/// propose or cancel shares and moves between caps.
public struct PartnerAdmin {}

/// One Blast partner. Shared, and read by immutable reference on every trade that names it. It has
/// no destroy: launches, presales, and vaults bind its ID for good and pay Blast's held fees only
/// with it, so deleting it would strand those fees. A zero share switches it off instead.
public struct Partner has key {
    id: UID,
    /// Where the partner's share is sent; set by the live cap's holder.
    recipient: address,
    /// The partner's share of Blast's fee on each trade, in basis points.
    share_bps: blast_admin::delayed::Delayed<ShareKey, u64>,
    /// The only cap that may set `recipient`; one cap may manage many partners.
    cap_id: blast_admin::delayed::Delayed<CapKey, ID>,
}

/// Keys a partner share's delayed lifecycle.
public struct ShareKey() has copy, drop, store;

/// Keys a partner managing cap's delayed lifecycle.
public struct CapKey() has copy, drop, store;

/// A company's authority over the recipient of every partner bound to it.
public struct PartnerCap has key, store {
    id: UID,
}
```

#### Functions

```move
/// Mints a cap. It controls nothing until Blast registers a partner under it,
/// so anyone may mint one, a company included.
public fun new_cap(ctx: &mut TxContext): PartnerCap

/// Registers and shares a partner managed by the cap `cap_id`, with its share
/// and managing cap in force immediately.
public fun register(
    _: &blast_admin::blast_acl::AdminWitness<PartnerAdmin>,
    cap_id: ID,
    recipient: address,
    share_bps: u64,
    ctx: &mut TxContext,
)

/// Proposes `share_bps` as the partner's share, applicable by anyone after the fixed
/// delay. A different proposal replaces a pending one and restarts the full delay;
/// `cancel_share` discards one, even after its delay has elapsed. Proposing the
/// applied or the pending share aborts.
public fun schedule_share(
    self: &mut Partner,
    _: &blast_admin::blast_acl::AdminWitness<PartnerAdmin>,
    share_bps: u64,
    clock: &Clock,
)

/// Applies the proposed share once its delay has elapsed. Anyone may call, the partner included.
public fun apply_share(self: &mut Partner, clock: &Clock)

/// Discards the proposed share, even a ready one, keeping the applied share.
public fun cancel_share(self: &mut Partner, _: &blast_admin::blast_acl::AdminWitness<PartnerAdmin>)

/// Proposes the cap `cap_id` to manage the partner, to replace a lost or stolen
/// cap, applicable by anyone after the fixed delay. The applied cap keeps working
/// until then. A different proposal replaces a pending one and restarts the full
/// delay; `cancel_cap` discards one, even after its delay has elapsed. Proposing
/// the applied or the pending cap aborts.
public fun schedule_cap(
    self: &mut Partner,
    _: &blast_admin::blast_acl::AdminWitness<PartnerAdmin>,
    cap_id: ID,
    clock: &Clock,
)

/// Applies the proposed managing cap once its delay has elapsed. Anyone may call.
public fun apply_cap(self: &mut Partner, clock: &Clock)

/// Discards the proposed managing cap, even a ready one, keeping the applied cap.
public fun cancel_cap(self: &mut Partner, _: &blast_admin::blast_acl::AdminWitness<PartnerAdmin>)

/// Sets where the partner is paid, at once. Only the applied cap may.
public fun set_recipient(self: &mut Partner, cap: &PartnerCap, recipient: address)

/// Destroys a cap, live or not.
public fun destroy_cap(cap: PartnerCap)

/// Transfers the partner its applied share of `fee` (Blast's fee on one trade) as a
/// coin, rounded down so Blast keeps the remainder, emits `PartnerPaid`, and returns
/// the amount sent. Nothing is sent or emitted while the share is zero. `fee`
/// keeps the rest. Anyone calling it pays the partner from their own balance.
public fun pay<T>(self: &Partner, fee: &mut Balance<T>, ctx: &mut TxContext): u64

/// The fixed delay every change Blast makes to a partner waits. Products use it
/// for their own delay, such as how long a partner they remove stays accepted.
public fun delay_ms(): u64

/// The largest share of Blast's fee one partner may earn, in basis points: 8,000.
public fun max_share_bps(): u64

/// Where the partner's share is sent now.
public fun recipient(self: &Partner): address

/// The applied share of Blast's fee, in basis points; `pay` uses it.
public fun share_bps(self: &Partner): u64

/// The proposed share with its ready time, if any. It changes nothing until applied.
public fun pending_share_bps(self: &Partner): Option<blast_admin::delayed::Proposal<u64>>

/// Whether `apply_share` would succeed now: a share is proposed, its ready time has passed, and
/// the fixed delay still fits in a Unix millisecond timestamp.
public fun is_share_ready(self: &Partner, clock: &Clock): bool

/// The ID of the applied cap, the only one that may set the recipient.
public fun cap_id(self: &Partner): ID

/// The proposed managing cap's ID with its ready time, if any. It changes nothing until applied.
public fun pending_cap_id(self: &Partner): Option<blast_admin::delayed::Proposal<ID>>

/// Whether `apply_cap` would succeed now: a cap is proposed, its ready time has passed, and the
/// fixed delay still fits in a Unix millisecond timestamp.
public fun is_cap_ready(self: &Partner, clock: &Clock): bool
```

#### Events

```move
/// Emitted at registration and on every recipient change: the partner's recipient.
/// Its share and cap events are `blast_admin::delayed`'s, owned by `partner_id`.
public struct PartnerUpdated has copy, drop {
    partner_id: ID,
    recipient: address,
}

/// Emitted whenever a partner is paid: `amount` of `T` sent to its recipient.
public struct PartnerPaid<phantom T> has copy, drop {
    partner_id: ID,
    amount: u64,
}
```

#### Constants

- `PARTNER_CHANGE_DELAY_MS: u64 = 7 * 24 * 60 * 60 * 1_000`: The fixed delay before a share or a cap Blast proposes can apply, and how long a partner a `partner_list::PartnerList` removes stays accepted.
- `MAX_SHARE_BPS: u64 = 8_000`: The largest share of Blast's fee one partner may earn: 80%.
- `BASIS_POINTS: u64 = 10_000`: Basis points in one whole.

### `blast_partners::partner_list`

The partners one Blast product accepts, one list per product.  A product embeds a `PartnerList` in the object holding its policy and alone decides who may change it: the list has `store` and no authority of its own, so only the embedding module can reach it mutably. Adding a partner accepts it at once. Removing one keeps it accepted for the fixed delay Blast gives for every change to a partner, `blast_partners::delay_ms()`, so anything built to name it keeps working that long. Sales and trades that already name a removed partner keep paying it; the list only gates naming it anew. Destroying the list ends every acceptance at once, without the delay, so a product destroys it only with the object that embeds it.

Source: https://github.com/interest-protocol/blast-v2-contracts/blob/main/sui/launch/partners/sources/partner_list.move

#### Types

```move
/// The partners one product accepts: each partner's ID maps to the time, in Unix epoch
/// milliseconds, it stops being accepted, `u64::MAX` until the product removes it. `parent` is the
/// object that embeds the list, named by its events.
public struct PartnerList has store {
    parent: ID,
    acceptance_ends_at_ms: Table<ID, u64>,
}
```

#### Functions

```move
/// An empty list embedded in `parent`.
public fun new(parent: &UID, ctx: &mut TxContext): PartnerList

/// Accepts `partner` at once. Adding a partner whose removal is pending cancels the removal;
/// adding a removed partner accepts it again. Aborts when it is already accepted with no removal
/// pending.
public fun add(self: &mut PartnerList, partner: &blast_partners::Partner)

/// Stops accepting `partner_id` once the fixed delay, `blast_partners::delay_ms()`, has passed
/// from now. Aborts unless the partner is accepted now with no removal pending.
public fun remove(self: &mut PartnerList, partner_id: ID, clock: &Clock)

/// Aborts unless the list accepts `partner_id` now: it was added, and any removal's delay has
/// not yet passed.
public fun assert_accepted(self: &PartnerList, partner_id: ID, clock: &Clock)

/// Deletes the list and every entry, accepted or removed, and emits `PartnerListDestroyed`. The
/// embedding module alone holds the list by value, so it alone decides when its acceptance ends.
public fun destroy(self: PartnerList)

/// Whether the list accepts `partner_id` now; `assert_accepted` aborts exactly when this is false.
public fun is_accepted(self: &PartnerList, partner_id: ID, clock: &Clock): bool

/// Whether the list has an entry for `partner_id`: accepted, removal pending, or removed.
public fun contains(self: &PartnerList, partner_id: ID): bool

/// When the list stops accepting `partner_id`, in Unix epoch milliseconds and exclusive:
/// `u64::MAX` until removed. Aborts in `sui::dynamic_field` unless the list `contains` it.
public fun acceptance_ends_at_ms(self: &PartnerList, partner_id: ID): u64

/// The number of entries, removed partners included.
public fun length(self: &PartnerList): u64

/// The object that embeds the list, named by its events.
public fun parent(self: &PartnerList): ID
```

#### Events

```move
/// The parent's list accepts the partner from now on.
public struct PartnerAdded has copy, drop {
    parent: ID,
    partner_id: ID,
}

/// The parent's list accepts the partner until `acceptance_ends_at_ms` (Unix epoch milliseconds),
/// exclusive.
public struct PartnerRemoved has copy, drop {
    parent: ID,
    partner_id: ID,
    acceptance_ends_at_ms: u64,
}

/// The parent's list was deleted: it accepts no partner from now on.
public struct PartnerListDestroyed has copy, drop {
    parent: ID,
}
```

### `blast_admin::blast_acl`

The one access-control list for every Blast package: one cold root and role-typed admins.  A role is a type that its consumer package declares. `AdminWitness<Role>` in a function signature is that function's role check, and a role from one package can never satisfy another package's signature.

Source: https://github.com/interest-protocol/blast-v2-contracts/blob/main/sui/governance/admin/sources/blast_acl.move

#### Types

```move
/// The shared singleton that lists the active root and every live administrator's roles. It has
/// no destructor: every consumer's sign-in reads it for the life of the protocol.
public struct ACL has key {
    id: UID,
    super_admin_cap_id: ID,
    admins: Table<ID, VecSet<TypeName>>,
    /// The nominated next super admin, acceptable once the fixed transfer delay passes.
    super_admin_transfer: delayed_action::DelayedAction<SuperAdminTransfer, address>,
}

/// Cold root authority for membership, roles, and delayed root replacement only.
public struct SuperAdminCap has key {
    id: UID,
}

/// Transferable, live-registry-checked administrator capability holding any granted roles.
public struct AdminCap has key, store {
    id: UID,
}

/// Keys the root transfer's delayed lifecycle events.
public struct SuperAdminTransfer() has copy, drop, store;

/// Transaction-local proof that a live `AdminCap` holding `Role` signed in.
public struct AdminWitness<phantom Role>() has drop;
```

#### Functions

```move
/// Creates and delivers one live administrator that holds no role yet.
public fun grant_admin(
    self: &mut ACL,
    root: &SuperAdminCap,
    recipient: address,
    ctx: &mut TxContext,
)

/// Revokes one live administrator and all its roles without requiring possession of its cap.
public fun revoke_admin(self: &mut ACL, root: &SuperAdminCap, admin_cap_id: ID)

/// Adds `Role` to a live administrator.
public fun grant_role<Role>(self: &mut ACL, root: &SuperAdminCap, admin_cap_id: ID)

/// Removes `Role` from a live administrator; an administrator with no role signs in to nothing.
public fun revoke_role<Role>(self: &mut ACL, root: &SuperAdminCap, admin_cap_id: ID)

/// Deletes an active or already-revoked administrator cap and reports what it still held.
public fun destroy_admin_cap(self: &mut ACL, admin_cap: AdminCap)

/// Nominates `recipient` as the next super admin; it may accept once the transfer delay
/// passes. A new nomination cancels a pending one and restarts the delay.
public fun begin_super_admin_transfer(
    self: &mut ACL,
    root: &SuperAdminCap,
    recipient: address,
    clock: &Clock,
    ctx: &mut TxContext,
)

/// Cancels the pending root transfer at once, even after its delay has passed.
public fun cancel_super_admin_transfer(self: &mut ACL, root: &SuperAdminCap)

/// Accepts a ready root transfer as its nominated recipient and invalidates the old cap.
public fun accept_super_admin_transfer(self: &mut ACL, clock: &Clock, ctx: &mut TxContext)

/// Deletes a root capability only after an accepted replacement made it inert.
public fun destroy_retired_super_admin_cap(self: &ACL, root: SuperAdminCap)

/// Proves that `admin_cap` is live and holds `Role`; the witness type is what consumers require.
public fun sign_in<Role>(self: &ACL, admin_cap: &AdminCap): AdminWitness<Role>

/// The ID of the one root cap that works; any other `SuperAdminCap` is inert.
public fun super_admin_cap_id(self: &ACL): ID

/// Whether `admin_cap_id` names a live administrator.
public fun is_admin(self: &ACL, admin_cap_id: ID): bool

/// Whether the live administrator `admin_cap_id` holds `Role`; false for an unknown cap.
public fun has_role<Role>(self: &ACL, admin_cap_id: ID): bool

/// The role type names, with defining IDs, a live administrator holds.
public fun admin_roles(self: &ACL, admin_cap_id: ID): vector<TypeName>

/// How many live administrators exist.
public fun admin_count(self: &ACL): u64

/// The root transfer's delayed slot: its fixed delay and any pending nominee.
public fun super_admin_transfer(
    self: &ACL,
): &delayed_action::DelayedAction<SuperAdminTransfer, address>

/// The most roles one administrator can hold.
public fun max_roles_per_admin(): u64

/// The fixed wait before a nominated super admin may accept the root, in milliseconds.
public fun super_admin_transfer_delay_ms(): u64
```

#### Constants

- `MAX_ROLES_PER_ADMIN: u64 = 32`
- `SUPER_ADMIN_TRANSFER_DELAY_MS: u64 = 7 * 24 * 60 * 60 * 1_000`: The wait before a nominated super admin may accept the root.

### `blast_admin::delayed`

Settings that change only after their applied delay, and the deadline rules every delayed type shares.  A timelock in the Compound/OpenZeppelin sense: schedule, wait the applied delay, then apply; cancellable until applied. Settings live here; one-shot actions in `delayed_action`, allowlists in `delayed_set` and keyed values in `delayed_map`.  Every value carries a module-defined key `K` and the ID of the object that stores it. Constructors take that object's `&UID`, which only the code creating or holding the object has, so no package can attribute a value or its events to another's object. Embedding modules use a struct without a public constructor as `K`, so only their module can pack it. Each delayed module emits every lifecycle fact of its type, generic in `K` and carrying `parent`; embedding modules emit only their own side effects.  Every change, a shorter delay included, waits the delay applied when it was scheduled. A delay can start at zero, so a schedule and its apply in one transaction then act as a setter; there is no other mutation path. No delay exceeds `max_delay_ms`, so a delay can always be changed again. Readers need no Clock.

Source: https://github.com/interest-protocol/blast-v2-contracts/blob/main/sui/governance/admin/sources/delayed/delayed.move

#### Types

```move
/// An applied setting, its applied delay and at most one proposed replacement.
public struct Delayed<K: copy + drop + store, T: copy + drop + store> has store {
    parent: ID,
    key: K,
    value: T,
    delay_ms: u64,
    pending: Option<Proposal<T>>,
}

/// A proposed value and the delay in force once it applies, ready from `ready_at_ms`.
public struct Proposal<T: copy + drop + store> has copy, drop, store {
    value: T,
    delay_ms: u64,
    ready_at_ms: u64,
}

/// A proposed next delay, ready from `ready_at_ms`; the delay stays representable at apply.
public struct PendingDelay has copy, drop, store {
    delay_ms: u64,
    ready_at_ms: u64,
}

/// A fixed deadline and the applied delay a change was scheduled under; a later delay change
/// never moves it.
public struct Deadline has copy, drop, store {
    delay_ms: u64,
    ready_at_ms: u64,
}
```

#### Functions

```move
/// A setting in force at once; every later change waits the applied delay.
public fun new<K: copy + drop + store, T: copy + drop + store>(
    key: K,
    parent: &UID,
    value: T,
    delay_ms: u64,
): Delayed<K, T>

/// The longest delay any delayed type accepts, in milliseconds.
public fun max_delay_ms(): u64

/// The applied value.
public fun value<K: copy + drop + store, T: copy + drop + store>(self: &Delayed<K, T>): T

/// The applied delay in milliseconds.
public fun delay_ms<K: copy + drop + store, T: copy + drop + store>(self: &Delayed<K, T>): u64

/// True when every change still to come waits a nonzero delay: the applied delay is nonzero
/// and no proposal would set it to zero.
public fun is_guarded<K: copy + drop + store, T: copy + drop + store>(self: &Delayed<K, T>): bool

/// The proposed replacement, if any.
public fun pending<K: copy + drop + store, T: copy + drop + store>(
    self: &Delayed<K, T>,
): Option<Proposal<T>>

/// Proposes `value` and the next delay `delay_ms`, ready after the applied delay, and
/// returns when. A replaced proposal is cancelled and the full applied delay restarts.
/// Aborts when the proposal would change neither the value nor the delay.
public fun schedule<K: copy + drop + store, T: copy + drop + store>(
    self: &mut Delayed<K, T>,
    value: T,
    delay_ms: u64,
    clock: &Clock,
): u64

/// Proposes only a new value, keeping the applied delay.
public fun schedule_value<K: copy + drop + store, T: copy + drop + store>(
    self: &mut Delayed<K, T>,
    value: T,
    clock: &Clock,
): u64

/// Proposes only a new delay, keeping the applied value. A shorter delay, zero included,
/// still waits the applied one.
public fun schedule_delay<K: copy + drop + store, T: copy + drop + store>(
    self: &mut Delayed<K, T>,
    delay_ms: u64,
    clock: &Clock,
): u64

/// Checks that `apply` would succeed now, changing nothing. Embedding modules call it before
/// settling accrued economics under the applied value in the same transaction.
public fun assert_ready<K: copy + drop + store, T: copy + drop + store>(
    self: &Delayed<K, T>,
    clock: &Clock,
)

/// Applies the ready proposal's value and delay together and returns the new value.
public fun apply<K: copy + drop + store, T: copy + drop + store>(
    self: &mut Delayed<K, T>,
    clock: &Clock,
): T

/// Discards the proposal, even a ready one, leaving the applied value and delay.
public fun cancel<K: copy + drop + store, T: copy + drop + store>(self: &mut Delayed<K, T>)

/// Deletes a setting with no proposal and records its end; cancel a proposal first.
public fun destroy<K: copy + drop + store, T: copy + drop + store>(self: Delayed<K, T>)

/// The proposed value, or the proposed change for an action slot.
public fun proposal_value<T: copy + drop + store>(self: &Proposal<T>): T

/// The delay in force once the proposal applies.
public fun proposal_delay_ms<T: copy + drop + store>(self: &Proposal<T>): u64

/// The Unix millisecond time from which the proposal can apply.
public fun proposal_ready_at_ms<T: copy + drop + store>(self: &Proposal<T>): u64

/// The proposed next delay.
public fun pending_delay_ms(self: &PendingDelay): u64

/// The Unix millisecond time from which the proposed delay can apply.
public fun pending_delay_ready_at_ms(self: &PendingDelay): u64

/// The applied delay the change waits, as its Scheduled event reported.
public fun deadline_delay_ms(self: &Deadline): u64

/// The Unix millisecond time from which the change can apply.
public fun deadline_ready_at_ms(self: &Deadline): u64

/// The ID of the object that stores this setting.
public fun parent<K: copy + drop + store, T: copy + drop + store>(self: &Delayed<K, T>): ID

/// The module-defined key of this setting.
public fun key<K: copy + drop + store, T: copy + drop + store>(self: &Delayed<K, T>): K

/// Whether `apply` would succeed now: a proposal is pending, its `ready_at_ms` has passed, and
/// its delay still fits in a Unix millisecond timestamp.
public fun is_ready<K: copy + drop + store, T: copy + drop + store>(
    self: &Delayed<K, T>,
    clock: &Clock,
): bool
```

#### Events

```move
/// A setting was created with its first applied value and delay.
public struct DelayedCreated<K: copy + drop + store, T: copy + drop + store> has copy, drop {
    parent: ID,
    key: K,
    value: T,
    delay_ms: u64,
}

/// A replacement value and next delay were proposed, ready from `ready_at_ms`.
public struct DelayedScheduled<K: copy + drop + store, T: copy + drop + store> has copy, drop {
    parent: ID,
    key: K,
    value: T,
    delay_ms: u64,
    ready_at_ms: u64,
}

/// A proposal was discarded, by cancellation or by a replacing schedule.
public struct DelayedCancelled<K: copy + drop + store, T: copy + drop + store> has copy, drop {
    parent: ID,
    key: K,
    value: T,
    delay_ms: u64,
    ready_at_ms: u64,
}

/// A ready proposal became the applied value and delay.
public struct DelayedApplied<K: copy + drop + store, T: copy + drop + store> has copy, drop {
    parent: ID,
    key: K,
    previous_value: T,
    value: T,
    delay_ms: u64,
}

/// A setting was deleted with nothing pending.
public struct DelayedDestroyed<K: copy + drop + store, phantom T: copy + drop + store> has copy, drop {
    parent: ID,
    key: K,
}
```

#### Constants

- `MAX_DELAY_MS: u64 = 30 * 24 * 60 * 60 * 1_000`: The longest delay any delayed value, action, set or map accepts: 30 days.

### `blast_admin::delayed_action`

One-shot actions, such as an upgrade or a capability reassignment, that run only after their slot's applied delay. The slot also governs its own delay. See `delayed` for the rules every delayed type shares.

Source: https://github.com/interest-protocol/blast-v2-contracts/blob/main/sui/governance/admin/sources/delayed/delayed_action.move

#### Types

```move
/// An applied delay and at most one proposed one-shot action or delay change.
public struct DelayedAction<K: copy + drop + store, A: copy + drop + store> has store {
    parent: ID,
    key: K,
    delay_ms: u64,
    pending: Option<delayed::Proposal<Change<A>>>,
}

/// What a proposal does once applied.
public enum Change<A: copy + drop + store> has copy, drop, store {
    /// Hands the embedding module its action; the delay stays as applied.
    Action(A),
    /// Changes only the delay, to the proposal's `delay_ms`.
    Delay,
}
```

#### Functions

```move
/// An action slot with nothing proposed; every action waits the applied delay.
public fun new<K: copy + drop + store, A: copy + drop + store>(
    key: K,
    parent: &UID,
    delay_ms: u64,
): DelayedAction<K, A>

/// The applied delay in milliseconds.
public fun delay_ms<K: copy + drop + store, A: copy + drop + store>(self: &DelayedAction<K, A>): u64

/// True when every action still to come waits a nonzero delay: the applied delay is nonzero
/// and no proposal would set it to zero.
public fun is_guarded<K: copy + drop + store, A: copy + drop + store>(
    self: &DelayedAction<K, A>,
): bool

/// The proposed action or delay change, if any.
public fun pending<K: copy + drop + store, A: copy + drop + store>(
    self: &DelayedAction<K, A>,
): Option<delayed::Proposal<Change<A>>>

/// Proposes `action`, ready after the applied delay, and returns when. A replaced
/// proposal is cancelled and the full applied delay restarts.
public fun schedule<K: copy + drop + store, A: copy + drop + store>(
    self: &mut DelayedAction<K, A>,
    action: A,
    clock: &Clock,
): u64

/// Proposes only a new delay, ready after the applied one, replacing any proposed action.
public fun schedule_delay<K: copy + drop + store, A: copy + drop + store>(
    self: &mut DelayedAction<K, A>,
    delay_ms: u64,
    clock: &Clock,
): u64

/// Consumes the ready proposed action and returns it for the embedding module to perform.
/// Aborts when the proposal is a delay change.
public fun take_action<K: copy + drop + store, A: copy + drop + store>(
    self: &mut DelayedAction<K, A>,
    clock: &Clock,
): A

/// Applies the ready proposed delay. Aborts when the proposal is an action.
public fun apply_delay<K: copy + drop + store, A: copy + drop + store>(
    self: &mut DelayedAction<K, A>,
    clock: &Clock,
)

/// Discards the proposal, even a ready one, leaving the applied delay.
public fun cancel<K: copy + drop + store, A: copy + drop + store>(self: &mut DelayedAction<K, A>)

/// Deletes an action slot with no proposal and records its end; cancel a proposal first.
public fun destroy<K: copy + drop + store, A: copy + drop + store>(self: DelayedAction<K, A>)

/// The proposed action, or none for a delay change.
public fun action<A: copy + drop + store>(self: &Change<A>): Option<A>

/// The proposed action, or none when nothing or a delay change is pending.
public fun pending_action<K: copy + drop + store, A: copy + drop + store>(
    self: &DelayedAction<K, A>,
): Option<A>

/// The proposed next delay and when it becomes ready, or none when nothing or an action is
/// pending.
public fun pending_delay<K: copy + drop + store, A: copy + drop + store>(
    self: &DelayedAction<K, A>,
): Option<delayed::PendingDelay>

/// Whether the pending proposal, an action or a delay change, would apply now.
public fun is_ready<K: copy + drop + store, A: copy + drop + store>(
    self: &DelayedAction<K, A>,
    clock: &Clock,
): bool

/// Whether a pending delay change would apply now.
public fun is_delay_ready<K: copy + drop + store, A: copy + drop + store>(
    self: &DelayedAction<K, A>,
    clock: &Clock,
): bool

/// The ID of the object that stores this action slot.
public fun parent<K: copy + drop + store, A: copy + drop + store>(self: &DelayedAction<K, A>): ID

/// The module-defined key of this action slot.
public fun key<K: copy + drop + store, A: copy + drop + store>(self: &DelayedAction<K, A>): K
```

#### Events

```move
/// An action slot was created with its first applied delay.
public struct ActionCreated<K: copy + drop + store, phantom A: copy + drop + store> has copy, drop {
    parent: ID,
    key: K,
    delay_ms: u64,
}

/// An action or delay change was proposed, ready from `ready_at_ms`; `delay_ms` is the
/// delay in force once it applies.
public struct ActionScheduled<K: copy + drop + store, A: copy + drop + store> has copy, drop {
    parent: ID,
    key: K,
    change: Change<A>,
    delay_ms: u64,
    ready_at_ms: u64,
}

    parent: ID,
    key: K,
    change: Change<A>,
    delay_ms: u64,
    ready_at_ms: u64,
}

/// A ready action was handed to its embedding module, or a delay change applied.
public struct ActionApplied<K: copy + drop + store, A: copy + drop + store> has copy, drop {
    parent: ID,
    key: K,
    change: Change<A>,
    delay_ms: u64,
}

/// An action slot was deleted with nothing pending.
public struct ActionDestroyed<K: copy + drop + store, phantom A: copy + drop + store> has copy, drop {
    parent: ID,
    key: K,
}
```

### `blast_admin::delayed_map`

Keyed values under one shared delay, such as per-coin overrides. Every entry change, setting or clearing, waits the delay applied when it was scheduled, and its deadline is fixed then; a later delay change never moves it. See `delayed` for the shared rules.

Source: https://github.com/interest-protocol/blast-v2-contracts/blob/main/sui/governance/admin/sources/delayed/delayed_map.move

#### Types

```move
/// Applied values by entry, one applied delay, at most one pending change per entry, and at
/// most one proposed delay. Values live in a Table, so reads cost one entry and the map
/// grows without bound; pending changes are few and live inline.
public struct DelayedMap<K: copy + drop + store, E: copy + drop + store, V: copy + drop + store> has store {
    parent: ID,
    key: K,
    entries: Table<E, V>,
    delay_ms: u64,
    pending: VecMap<E, EntryProposal<V>>,
    pending_delay: Option<delayed::PendingDelay>,
}

/// A proposed entry value, or none to clear the entry, and its fixed deadline.
public struct EntryProposal<V: copy + drop + store> has copy, drop, store {
    value: Option<V>,
    deadline: delayed::Deadline,
}

/// What a map proposal does once applied.
public enum MapChange<E: copy + drop + store, V: copy + drop + store> has copy, drop, store {
    /// Sets the entry to `value`, or clears it when `value` is none.
    Entry {
        entry: E,
        value: Option<V>,
    },
    /// Changes the delay for later entry changes.
    Delay,
}
```

#### Functions

```move
/// An empty map; every entry change waits the applied delay.

    key: K,
    parent: &UID,

    ctx: &mut TxContext,
): DelayedMap<K, E, V>

    self: &DelayedMap<K, E, V>,
    entry: &E,
): Option<V>

    self: &DelayedMap<K, E, V>,
): u64

    entry: &E,
): Option<EntryProposal<V>>

    clock: &Clock,

): u64

/// Applies the ready proposed delay to later entry changes.
public fun apply_delay<K: copy + drop + store, E: copy + drop + store, V: copy + drop + store>(
    self: &mut DelayedMap<K, E, V>,
    clock: &Clock,
)

/// Discards the proposed delay, even a ready one.
public fun cancel_delay<K: copy + drop + store, E: copy + drop + store, V: copy + drop + store>(
    self: &mut DelayedMap<K, E, V>,
)

/// Deletes a map with no pending entry or delay change and records its end, which also
/// ends every remaining entry.
public fun destroy<K: copy + drop + store, E: copy + drop + store, V: copy + drop + store>(
    self: DelayedMap<K, E, V>,
)

/// The proposed value, or none when the change clears the entry.
public fun entry_proposal_value<V: copy + drop + store>(self: &EntryProposal<V>): Option<V>

/// The deadline the change waits.

    self: &DelayedMap<K, E, V>,

    self: &DelayedMap<K, E, V>,
): K

public fun contains<K: copy + drop + store, E: copy + drop + store, V: copy + drop + store>(
    self: &DelayedMap<K, E, V>,
    entry: &E,

public fun length<K: copy + drop + store, E: copy + drop + store, V: copy + drop + store>(
    self: &DelayedMap<K, E, V>,

public fun pending_count<K: copy + drop + store, E: copy + drop + store, V: copy + drop + store>(
    self: &DelayedMap<K, E, V>,

/// no proposal would set it to zero, and no entry change scheduled earlier is pending.
public fun is_guarded<K: copy + drop + store, E: copy + drop + store, V: copy + drop + store>(
    self: &DelayedMap<K, E, V>,
): bool

public fun changed_entry<E: copy + drop + store, V: copy + drop + store>(
    self: &MapChange<E, V>,
): Option<E>

/// The value an entry change proposes, whose inner none clears the entry, or none for a delay
/// change.
public fun changed_value<E: copy + drop + store, V: copy + drop + store>(
    self: &MapChange<E, V>,
): Option<Option<V>>
```

### `blast_admin::delayed_set`

Allowlists whose members join only after the applied delay and leave at once. See `delayed` for the rules every delayed type shares.

Source: https://github.com/interest-protocol/blast-v2-contracts/blob/main/sui/governance/admin/sources/delayed/delayed_set.move

#### Types

```move
/// Admitted members, one applied delay for new admissions, pending admissions with fixed
/// deadlines, and at most one proposed delay. A delay change never moves a deadline.
public struct DelayedSet<K: copy + drop + store, E: copy + drop + store> has store {
    parent: ID,
    key: K,
    members: VecSet<E>,
    delay_ms: u64,
    admissions: VecMap<E, delayed::Deadline>,
    pending_delay: Option<delayed::PendingDelay>,
}

/// What a set proposal does once applied.
public enum SetChange<E: copy + drop + store> has copy, drop, store {
    /// Admits the member.
    Admission(E),
    /// Changes the delay for later admissions.
    Delay,
}
```

#### Functions

```move
/// An empty set; every admission waits the applied delay.
public fun new<K: copy + drop + store, E: copy + drop + store>(
    key: K,
    parent: &UID,
    delay_ms: u64,
): DelayedSet<K, E>

public fun contains<K: copy + drop + store, E: copy + drop + store>(
    self: &DelayedSet<K, E>,
    member: &E,
): bool

public fun members<K: copy + drop + store, E: copy + drop + store>(
    self: &DelayedSet<K, E>,
): VecSet<E>

public fun delay_ms<K: copy + drop + store, E: copy + drop + store>(self: &DelayedSet<K, E>): u64

public fun admission_ready_at_ms<K: copy + drop + store, E: copy + drop + store>(
    self: &DelayedSet<K, E>,
    member: &E,
): Option<u64>

    self: &DelayedSet<K, E>,

public fun pending_delay<K: copy + drop + store, E: copy + drop + store>(
    self: &DelayedSet<K, E>,
): Option<delayed::PendingDelay>

/// when. Rescheduling a pending member cancels its admission and restarts the delay.
/// Aborts when `member` is already admitted.
public fun schedule_add<K: copy + drop + store, E: copy + drop + store>(
    self: &mut DelayedSet<K, E>,
    member: E,
    clock: &Clock,
): u64

    self: &mut DelayedSet<K, E>,
    member: E,
    clock: &Clock,
)

public fun cancel_add<K: copy + drop + store, E: copy + drop + store>(
    self: &mut DelayedSet<K, E>,
    member: E,
)

/// Removes `member` at once and discards its pending admission, leaving the delay.
/// Aborts when `member` is neither admitted nor pending.
public fun remove<K: copy + drop + store, E: copy + drop + store>(
    self: &mut DelayedSet<K, E>,
    member: E,
)

/// admissions keep their deadlines.

    self: &mut DelayedSet<K, E>,
    delay_ms: u64,
    clock: &Clock,
): u64

/// Applies the ready proposed delay to later admissions.
public fun apply_delay<K: copy + drop + store, E: copy + drop + store>(
    self: &mut DelayedSet<K, E>,
    clock: &Clock,
)

/// Discards the proposed delay, even a ready one.
public fun cancel_delay<K: copy + drop + store, E: copy + drop + store>(self: &mut DelayedSet<K, E>)

/// Deletes a set with no pending admission or delay change and records its end, which
/// also ends every remaining membership.
public fun destroy<K: copy + drop + store, E: copy + drop + store>(self: DelayedSet<K, E>)

/// How many members are admitted.
public fun length<K: copy + drop + store, E: copy + drop + store>(self: &DelayedSet<K, E>): u64

/// Whether `member` has a pending admission.
public fun is_pending<K: copy + drop + store, E: copy + drop + store>(
    self: &DelayedSet<K, E>,
    member: &E,
): bool

/// Whether `member`'s pending admission would apply now.
public fun is_ready<K: copy + drop + store, E: copy + drop + store>(
    self: &DelayedSet<K, E>,
    member: &E,
    clock: &Clock,
): bool

/// Whether a pending delay change would apply now.
public fun is_delay_ready<K: copy + drop + store, E: copy + drop + store>(
    self: &DelayedSet<K, E>,
    clock: &Clock,
): bool

/// The ID of the object that stores this set.
public fun parent<K: copy + drop + store, E: copy + drop + store>(self: &DelayedSet<K, E>): ID

/// The module-defined key of this set.
public fun key<K: copy + drop + store, E: copy + drop + store>(self: &DelayedSet<K, E>): K

/// The deadline of `member`'s pending admission, if one is pending.
public fun admission_deadline<K: copy + drop + store, E: copy + drop + store>(
    self: &DelayedSet<K, E>,
    member: &E,
): Option<delayed::Deadline>

/// The member a change admits, or none for a delay change.
public fun member<E: copy + drop + store>(self: &SetChange<E>): Option<E>
```

#### Events

```move
}

/// A member left the set at once.
public struct MemberRemoved<K: copy + drop + store, E: copy + drop + store> has copy, drop {
    parent: ID,
    key: K,
    member: E,
}

/// A set was deleted with nothing pending; its remaining members leave with it.
public struct SetDestroyed<K: copy + drop + store, phantom E: copy + drop + store> has copy, drop {
    parent: ID,
    key: K,
}
```

### `blast_admin::package_admin`

One package's sealed Publisher and UpgradeCap, its delayed upgrade decisions, and the role-gated pause controls of its `PackageVersion`, typed by the package's one-time witness.  The native capabilities never leave the shared `PackageAdmin`: only the package itself, with its package witness, borrows the Publisher by immutable reference, nothing lends the UpgradeCap, and the cap acts only through a scheduled upgrade or the one terminal `make_immutable`. Each role is generic over the package's OTW, so the ACL stores `UpgradeAdmin<A>` and `UpgradeAdmin<B>` as distinct grants and one package's administrator never reaches another's PackageAdmin. This package is immutable: no consumer bytecode version gates these calls, and a consumer whose new bytecode compiles the wrong version can still schedule a repair.

Source: https://github.com/interest-protocol/blast-v2-contracts/blob/main/sui/governance/admin/sources/package/package_admin.move

#### Types

```move
/// The exact code digest or irreversible end of upgrades approved for execution.
public enum UpgradeAction has copy, drop, store {
    Upgrade(vector<u8>),
    MakeImmutable,
}

/// Keys the delayed lifecycle of one package's upgrade actions and their delay.
public struct UpgradeKey<phantom OTW: drop>() has copy, drop, store;

/// One package's shared administration, sealing its framework Publisher and UpgradeCap. It has no destructor:
/// deleting it would release or strand the native capabilities it seals.
public struct PackageAdmin<phantom OTW: drop> has key {
    id: UID,
    publisher: Publisher,
    /// Native upgrade authority; `none` once the package is immutable.
    upgrade_cap: Option<UpgradeCap>,
    upgrade: delayed_action::DelayedAction<UpgradeKey<OTW>, UpgradeAction>,
}

/// Schedules and executes the package's upgrades and immutability, and changes their delay.
public struct UpgradeAdmin<phantom OTW> {}

/// May set the package's pause bits immediately, never clear them.
public struct PauseAdmin<phantom OTW> {}

/// May clear the package's pause bits, only through the package's own unpause.
public struct UnpauseAdmin<phantom OTW> {}

/// May cancel the package's scheduled upgrade action or delay change.
public struct CancelAdmin<phantom OTW> {}
```

#### Functions

```move
/// Consumes the unique publication proof, seals the never-upgraded native cap of the OTW's
/// package beside its Publisher, and shares both the administration and the package version.
/// The version starts with every defined bit paused; an `UnpauseAdmin` releases it through the
/// package's own unpause.
public fun wrap<OTW: drop>(
    publication: package_version::Publication<OTW>,
    upgrade_cap: UpgradeCap,
    ctx: &mut TxContext,
)

/// Schedules one exact code digest under the applied delay, replacing any previous action.
/// Changing the delay requires a separate delay-only proposal. Aborts once the package is
/// immutable.
public fun schedule_upgrade<OTW: drop>(
    self: &mut PackageAdmin<OTW>,
    _: &blast_acl::AdminWitness<UpgradeAdmin<OTW>>,
    digest: vector<u8>,
    clock: &Clock,
)

/// Schedules the irreversible destruction of upgrade authority under the applied delay.
/// Aborts once the package is immutable.
public fun schedule_make_immutable<OTW: drop>(
    self: &mut PackageAdmin<OTW>,
    _: &blast_acl::AdminWitness<UpgradeAdmin<OTW>>,
    clock: &Clock,
)

/// Cancels the scheduled action or delay change, even after its execution threshold.
public fun cancel_upgrade_action<OTW: drop>(
    self: &mut PackageAdmin<OTW>,
    _: &blast_acl::AdminWitness<CancelAdmin<OTW>>,
)

/// Consumes the ready upgrade action for exactly `digest` and authorizes it under the sealed
/// cap's current policy. A scheduled upgrade implies the cap: scheduling requires it, and only a
/// consumed immutability action removes it.
public fun authorize_upgrade<OTW: drop>(
    self: &mut PackageAdmin<OTW>,
    _: &blast_acl::AdminWitness<UpgradeAdmin<OTW>>,
    digest: vector<u8>,
    clock: &Clock,
): UpgradeTicket

/// Commits the native receipt into the sealed cap that authorized it, then advances the package
/// version by one in the same call. The new bytecode must compile that version; bytecode
/// compiling any other fails every `assert_current`, while these calls stay available to schedule
/// a repair.
public fun commit_upgrade_and_advance<OTW: drop>(
    self: &mut PackageAdmin<OTW>,
    _: &blast_acl::AdminWitness<UpgradeAdmin<OTW>>,
    package_version: &mut package_version::PackageVersion<OTW>,
    receipt: UpgradeReceipt,
)

/// Consumes the ready immutability action and irreversibly destroys native upgrade authority;
/// the Publisher stays sealed. This is the one terminal upgrade action: the cap's policy is
/// never narrowed in between. The package itself must pass its package witness (aborts with
/// `EWrongPackageWitness` otherwise), so only its own code can end its upgrades. Each package
/// that may end its upgrades wraps this call in a thin function that first runs
/// `package_version.assert_current(constants::version!())`, so only its live bytecode can.
public fun make_immutable<OTW: drop, PackageWitness: drop>(
    self: &mut PackageAdmin<OTW>,
    _: &blast_acl::AdminWitness<UpgradeAdmin<OTW>>,
    _: PackageWitness,
    package_version: &package_version::PackageVersion<OTW>,
    clock: &Clock,
)

/// Proposes only a new upgrade delay, ready after the applied one; replaces any pending
/// upgrade or immutability action.
public fun schedule_upgrade_delay<OTW: drop>(
    self: &mut PackageAdmin<OTW>,
    _: &blast_acl::AdminWitness<UpgradeAdmin<OTW>>,
    delay_ms: u64,
    clock: &Clock,
)

/// Applies a ready delay-only proposal; anyone may call it, and it cannot consume an action.
public fun apply_upgrade_delay<OTW: drop>(self: &mut PackageAdmin<OTW>, clock: &Clock)

/// Sets the requested pause bits immediately.
public fun pause<OTW: drop>(
    package_version: &mut package_version::PackageVersion<OTW>,
    _: &blast_acl::AdminWitness<PauseAdmin<OTW>>,
    bits: u8,
)

/// Clears the requested pause bits for the package's own unpause, which first checks its own
/// configuration delays and then passes a value of its package witness, the type pinned at
/// publication. Aborts while any upgrade delay still to come could be zero. Only the package's
/// own code constructs that type, so a role alone cannot skip its checks.
public fun clear_pause_bits<OTW: drop, PackageWitness: drop>(
    self: &PackageAdmin<OTW>,
    _: &blast_acl::AdminWitness<UnpauseAdmin<OTW>>,
    _: PackageWitness,
    package_version: &mut package_version::PackageVersion<OTW>,
    bits: u8,
)

/// Borrows the sealed Publisher for package `OTW` itself, which passes a value of its
/// package witness, the type pinned at publication, to create Display or transfer policies for
/// its own types. The reference cannot move, mutate or burn the Publisher.
public fun publisher<OTW: drop, PackageWitness: drop>(
    self: &PackageAdmin<OTW>,
    _: PackageWitness,
    package_version: &package_version::PackageVersion<OTW>,
): &Publisher

/// Borrows the sealed Publisher mutably for package `OTW` itself, gated exactly like
/// `publisher` (aborts with `EWrongPackageWitness`), for framework calls that take
/// `&mut Publisher` such as `sui::display_registry::new_with_publisher`. A Publisher has no
/// `drop`, so a mutable reference cannot overwrite it, and the pinned framework has no call that
/// moves a value out from behind one. If a later framework adds a generic swap, only the package
/// holding its witness could use it, and the Publisher would then leave this PackageAdmin.
public fun publisher_mut<OTW: drop, PackageWitness: drop>(
    self: &mut PackageAdmin<OTW>,
    _: PackageWitness,
    package_version: &package_version::PackageVersion<OTW>,
): &mut Publisher

/// The applied upgrade delay, in milliseconds.
public fun upgrade_delay_ms<OTW: drop>(self: &PackageAdmin<OTW>): u64

/// The pending upgrade action or delay change, if any.
public fun pending_upgrade<OTW: drop>(
    self: &PackageAdmin<OTW>,
): Option<delayed::Proposal<delayed_action::Change<UpgradeAction>>>

/// True when every upgrade action still to come waits a nonzero delay.
public fun is_upgrade_delay_guarded<OTW: drop>(self: &PackageAdmin<OTW>): bool

/// Whether native upgrade authority remains; false once the package is immutable.
public fun has_upgrade_cap<OTW: drop>(self: &PackageAdmin<OTW>): bool

/// The sealed UpgradeCap's ID, or none once the package is immutable.
public fun upgrade_cap_id<OTW: drop>(self: &PackageAdmin<OTW>): Option<ID>

/// The package ID the next upgrade starts from, or none once the package is immutable.
public fun current_package_id<OTW: drop>(self: &PackageAdmin<OTW>): Option<ID>

/// The native upgrade count plus one, or none once the package is immutable.
public fun upgrade_cap_version<OTW: drop>(self: &PackageAdmin<OTW>): Option<u64>

/// The native upgrade policy, or none once the package is immutable.
public fun upgrade_policy<OTW: drop>(self: &PackageAdmin<OTW>): Option<u8>

/// The sealed Publisher's ID.
public fun publisher_id<OTW: drop>(self: &PackageAdmin<OTW>): ID

/// The digest an upgrade action approves, or none for immutability.
public fun upgrade_digest(self: &UpgradeAction): Option<vector<u8>>

/// The delay every package's upgrade actions wait from wrap until changed, in milliseconds.
public fun initial_upgrade_delay_ms(): u64
```

#### Constants

- `INITIAL_UPGRADE_DELAY_MS: u64 = 48 * 60 * 60 * 1_000`: The delay every upgrade or immutability action waits from publication until changed.
- `PACKAGE_DIGEST_LENGTH: u64 = 32`: The byte length of a package digest, the only length `schedule_upgrade` accepts.
- `INITIAL_UPGRADE_CAP_VERSION: u64 = 1`: The framework stamps a freshly published package's `UpgradeCap` with version one.

### `blast_admin::package_version`

One package's publication proof, version and pause bits, typed by its one-time witness.  The consumer's initializer turns its one-time witness into an owned `Publication`, which `package_admin::wrap` later converts into the shared `PackageVersion`, fully paused at version one. The consumer compiles its own version constant and checks it with `assert_current`; it also chooses which pause bits exist and pins its package witness, the one type whose value its own code passes to clear pause bits or to borrow its Publisher. Setting and clearing bits and advancing the version are sealed here and reached only through `package_admin`'s role-gated calls, so no package can build a second version or move a version by any other path.

Source: https://github.com/interest-protocol/blast-v2-contracts/blob/main/sui/governance/admin/sources/package/package_version.move

#### Types

```move
/// Owned publication custody; it cannot supply the `PackageVersion` that operations require. It has
/// no destructor: it holds the package's Publisher, whose one exit is `package_admin::wrap`.
public struct Publication<phantom OTW: drop> has key {
    id: UID,
    publisher: Publisher,
    valid_pause_bits: u8,
    package_witness: TypeName,
}

/// The package's shared version and pause bits. It has no destructor: every
/// version-gated call of the package reads it.
public struct PackageVersion<phantom OTW: drop> has key {
    id: UID,
    /// The bits this package defines; every pause or unpause request must fall within them.
    valid_pause_bits: u8,
    paused: u8,
    /// The package witness: the exact type, with defining IDs, whose value only the package's own
    /// code passes to `package_admin::clear_pause_bits`, `package_admin::publisher` and
    /// `package_admin::make_immutable`.
    package_witness: TypeName,
    /// The only version whose compiled bytecode passes `assert_current`.
    version: u64,
}
```

#### Functions

```move
/// Claims the Publisher from the package's one-time witness, so a publication can only come into
/// existence inside that package's initializer; the framework rejects any other witness.
/// `valid_pause_bits` fixes the bits the package defines, and zero defines none.
/// `PackageWitness` pins the package witness, the one type that clears those bits and borrows the
/// Publisher: a struct the package defines, other than its OTW, that only the package's own
/// code constructs and that it never returns or stores.
public fun new_publication<OTW: drop, PackageWitness: drop>(
    otw: OTW,
    valid_pause_bits: u8,
    ctx: &mut TxContext,
): Publication<OTW>

/// Transfers the publication to the transaction sender, who later wraps it with the
/// package's `UpgradeCap`.
public fun keep<OTW: drop>(self: Publication<OTW>, ctx: &mut TxContext)

/// Rejects callable bytecode whose compiled version is not the active one.
public fun assert_current<OTW: drop>(self: &PackageVersion<OTW>, compiled_version: u64)

/// Rejects an operation whose pause bit is set, before its lifecycle or economic checks. This is a
/// mask check only: a bit the package never defined is never set, so it never aborts.
public fun assert_not_paused<OTW: drop>(self: &PackageVersion<OTW>, bit: u8)

/// The active version; only bytecode compiling it passes `assert_current`.
public fun version<OTW: drop>(self: &PackageVersion<OTW>): u64

/// The pause bits the package defines.
public fun valid_pause_bits<OTW: drop>(self: &PackageVersion<OTW>): u8

/// The pause bits currently set.
public fun paused<OTW: drop>(self: &PackageVersion<OTW>): u8

/// True when any of `bits` is set.
public fun is_paused<OTW: drop>(self: &PackageVersion<OTW>, bits: u8): bool

/// The package witness type, with defining IDs, pinned at publication.
public fun package_witness<OTW: drop>(self: &PackageVersion<OTW>): TypeName

/// The pause bits the publication will define once wrapped.
public fun publication_valid_pause_bits<OTW: drop>(self: &Publication<OTW>): u8

/// The ID of the Publisher the publication holds.
public fun publication_publisher_id<OTW: drop>(self: &Publication<OTW>): ID

/// The package witness type, with defining IDs, the publication pins.
public fun publication_package_witness<OTW: drop>(self: &Publication<OTW>): TypeName
```

#### Constants

- `INITIAL_VERSION: u64 = 1`
